2025/26 S2

April / May 2026

Q1：随机接入协议点评、统计复用概率、停等 ARQ。Q2：Checksum + CRC、三个 C 类的 FLSM/VLSM。

25S2 Q1(a)四种随机接入协议的效率和时延8 分 · 文字
Briefly comment about the efficiency and delay performance of: (i) Slotted ALOHA (ii) 1-persistent CSMA (iii) Non-persistent CSMA (iv) CSMA/CD.
简要评述下面几种协议的效率和时延性能：(i) 时隙 ALOHA；(ii) 1-坚持 CSMA；(iii) 非坚持 CSMA；(iv) CSMA/CD。
参考答案
  • Slotted ALOHA：只能在时隙开头发，脆弱期从 2F 缩短到 F，最大吞吐量 S = 1/e ≈ 0.368（G = 1 时），是纯 ALOHA 的两倍。效率与 a 无关，但低负载时也要等到下一个时隙边界才能发，有平均半个时隙的额外时延；负载过高时碰撞和重传越滚越多，吞吐量趋于 0，时延趋于无穷。
  • 1-persistent CSMA：一直侦听，信道一空闲立刻发（最贪心）。时延低（不浪费空闲时间），但多个等待的站会在信道空闲的同一时刻一起发，碰撞多，效率低（a = 0.01 时最大约 0.53）。
  • Non-persistent CSMA：信道忙就随机退避一段时间再侦听（最不贪心）。把等待的站在时间上打散，碰撞少，效率高（a = 0.01 时约 0.81），但信道空闲时可能大家都在退避，时延高。
  • CSMA/CD：在 CSMA 基础上边发边听，最多 2tprop 就能发现碰撞并中止，浪费的只是检测时间而不是整帧。a 小时效率最高：ρmax = 1/(1 + 6.44a)；时延也小。但 a 增大时效率快速下降，a 接近 1 时不如 ALOHA 类协议。

总结：CSMA 一族的性能都依赖 a = tprop/tframe，a 越小越好；ALOHA 类不依赖 a。

English answer

  • Slotted ALOHA: can only transmit at the start of a slot, so the vulnerable period shrinks from 2F to F; maximum throughput S = 1/e ≈ 0.368 (at G = 1), twice that of pure ALOHA. Efficiency does not depend on a, but at low load a station must still wait for the next slot boundary, adding an average half-slot delay; at high load collisions and retransmissions snowball, throughput tends to 0 and delay tends to infinity.
  • 1-persistent CSMA: keeps sensing and transmits immediately once the channel is idle (the greediest policy). Delay is low (no idle time is wasted), but several waiting stations transmit at the same instant the channel goes idle, so collisions are frequent and efficiency is low (maximum about 0.53 at a = 0.01).
  • Non-persistent CSMA: if the channel is busy, backs off for a random time before sensing again (the least greedy policy). This spreads waiting stations out in time, so collisions are fewer and efficiency is higher (about 0.81 at a = 0.01), but the channel can sit idle while everyone is backing off, giving higher delay.
  • CSMA/CD: listens while transmitting on top of CSMA, so a collision is detected and aborted within at most 2tprop; only detection time is wasted, not the whole frame. Efficiency is highest when a is small: ρmax = 1/(1 + 6.44a); delay is also small. But efficiency drops quickly as a grows, and near a = 1 it is worse than the ALOHA family.

Summary: the whole CSMA family depends on a = tprop/tframe — smaller a is always better; the ALOHA family does not depend on a.

25S2 Q1(b)分组交换网络"忙"的概率8 分 · 推导
A packet-switching network with a single output of R bps is shared by P users. Each user is active with probability A and requires T bps while active. Derive the probability that one of the P users plans to transmit but finds the network busy (a formula containing a sum of probabilities).
附加练习：代入 R = 1 Mbps、T = 100 kbps、P = 35、A = 0.1 算出数值。
提示

链路最多同时服务 N = ⌊R/T⌋ 人。"我"想发时网络忙，意思是其他 P − 1 个人里已经有至少 N 个在发。

完整解答
链路同时能服务 N = ⌊R / T⌋ 个活跃用户其他 P − 1 个用户中恰有 k 人活跃：C(P−1, k) · Ak · (1 − A)P−1−k用户想发时网络忙 ⇔ 其他人中至少 N 人活跃：P(busy | 想发) = Σk=NP−1 C(P−1, k) Ak (1 − A)P−1−k若要"该用户想发 且 遇到忙"的联合概率，再乘以 A

代入 N = 10、P − 1 = 34、A = 0.1：Σk=1034 C(34,k)·0.1k·0.934−k ≈ 0.00137（联合概率 ≈ 1.37×10−4）。对比 slide 27 的"35 人中超过 10 人活跃"概率 0.0004：这里条件是"我已经在用"，所以门槛是其他人 ≥ 10，而不是 > 10。

English answer

  • The link can serve at most N = ⌊R / T⌋ active users at the same time.
  • Probability that exactly k of the other P − 1 users are active: C(P−1, k) · Ak · (1 − A)P−1−k.
  • The network is busy when this user wants to transmit ⇔ at least N of the other users are already active:
  • P(busy | wants to transmit) = Σk=NP−1 C(P−1, k) Ak (1 − A)P−1−k
  • For the joint probability "this user wants to transmit AND finds the network busy", multiply by A as well.

Substituting N = 10, P − 1 = 34, A = 0.1: Σk=1034 C(34,k)·0.1k·0.934−k ≈ 0.00137 (joint probability ≈ 1.37×10−4). Compare with the slide 27 case "more than 10 of 35 active" = 0.0004: here the condition is "I am already trying to send", so the threshold on the others is ≥ 10, not > 10.

25S2 Q1(c)停等 ARQ 的平均时延和效率9 分
S 用停等 ARQ 连续发 1200 字节数据帧（含 200 字节首部）给 R。信道 400 Mbps，BER = 5×10−5，链路 5 km，传播速度 2×108 m/s。R 对每个成功收到的帧回一个 200 字节 ACK。求成功传一帧的平均总时延，以及数据载荷传输的效率。
提示

一个发送周期 = tf + tprop + tack + tprop。帧出错要整个周期重来，平均发 Nr = 1/(1 − p) 次。按讲义假设 ACK 不出错。

完整解答
tf = 1200 × 8 / (400×106) = 24 µs；tack = 1600 / (400×106) = 4 µstprop = 5000 / (2×108) = 25 µs一个周期 = 24 + 25 + 4 + 25 = 78 µsp = 1 − (1 − 5×10−5)9600 = 0.3812 → Nr = 1/(1 − p) = 1.6161平均总时延 = Nr × 78 = 126.06 µs每次成功送出 1000 字节载荷，载荷时间 = 8000 / (400×106) = 20 µs载荷效率 = 20 / 126.06 = 15.87%（按整帧算 24/126.06 = 19.04%）
另一种口径：如果 ACK 也按 BER 出错，成功概率 = (1 − 5×10−5)9600+1600 = 0.5712，平均时延 = 78 / 0.5712 = 136.6 µs，效率 14.65%。讲义 slide 39 明确假设 "ACKs and NAKs are never in error"，按上面的答案写，并注明假设。

English answer

  • tf = 1200 × 8 / (400×106) = 24 µs; tack = 1600 / (400×106) = 4 µs
  • tprop = 5000 / (2×108) = 25 µs
  • One cycle = 24 + 25 + 4 + 25 = 78 µs
  • p = 1 − (1 − 5×10−5)9600 = 0.3812 → Nr = 1/(1 − p) = 1.6161
  • Average total delay = Nr × 78 = 126.06 µs
  • Each success delivers 1000 bytes of payload; payload time = 8000 / (400×106) = 20 µs
  • Payload efficiency = 20 / 126.06 = 15.87% (if computed on the whole frame: 24/126.06 = 19.04%)

Alternative assumption: if the ACK can also be corrupted by the BER, success probability = (1 − 5×10−5)9600+1600 = 0.5712, average delay = 78 / 0.5712 = 136.6 µs, efficiency 14.65%. The notes (slide 39) explicitly assume "ACKs and NAKs are never in error", so give the answer above and state that assumption.

25S2 Q2(a)Checksum 与 CRC（x³ + 1）10 分
Amy 发送 12 位数据 1001 0100 1010。(i) 4 位块长的 Checksum；(ii) 生成多项式 g(x) = x³ + 1 的 CRC 码字；(iii) 比较两者的检错能力。
提示

(i) 1001 = 9，0100 = 4，1010 = 10，求和后 mod 15。(ii) x³ + 1 = 1001，在数据后补 3 个 0 再做模 2 除法。

完整解答

(i) Checksum

9 + 4 + 10 = 23；23 mod 15 = 8checksum = 15 − 8 = 7 = 0111发送 1001 0100 1010 0111；验算 9 + 4 + 10 + 7 = 30 ≡ 0 (mod 15) ✓

(ii) CRC

M(x)·x³ = 100101001010 000，除以 1001余数 R = 010码字 = 100101001010 010

可以在学习手册的 CRC 长除法实验里选"25/26 S2"预设看每一步。

(iii) 比较

Checksum 只把各字的值加起来，不看顺序：字的顺序调换、插入删除全 0 字、互相抵消的多位错误都检测不到；优点是软件实现简单（所以用于每跳重算的 IP 首部）。CRC 用多项式除法，同时考虑值和位置，检错能力由生成多项式决定：x³ + 1 有 2 项，能检测所有单比特错误（P1）；x³+1 = (x+1)(x²+x+1) 含 (x+1)，能检测所有奇数个错误（P2）；3 次，能检测所有长度 ≤ 3 的突发（P4）；但它整除 x³ + 1，间隔为 3 的双比特错误检测不到（P3 不满足）。总体上 CRC 比 checksum 强得多，适合用硬件（移位寄存器）在链路层实现。

English answer

(i) Checksum

  • 9 + 4 + 10 = 23; 23 mod 15 = 8
  • checksum = 15 − 8 = 7 = 0111
  • Transmitted word: 1001 0100 1010 0111; check: 9 + 4 + 10 + 7 = 30 ≡ 0 (mod 15) ✓

(ii) CRC

  • M(x)·x3 = 100101001010 000, divide by 1001
  • Remainder R = 010
  • Codeword = 100101001010 010

You can walk through every step of this long division using the CRC long-division lab in the study guide (choose the "25/26 S2" preset).

(iii) Comparison

Checksum only adds up the values of the words and ignores their order: swapping the order of words, inserting or deleting all-zero words, or multi-bit errors that cancel each other out all go undetected; its advantage is a simple software implementation (which is why it is used in the IP header, recomputed at every hop). CRC uses polynomial division, which accounts for both value and position; its detection power depends on the generator polynomial: x3 + 1 has 2 terms, so it detects all single-bit errors (P1); x3 + 1 = (x + 1)(x2 + x + 1) contains the factor (x + 1), so it detects all odd numbers of errors (P2); it has degree 3, so it detects all burst errors of length ≤ 3 (P4); but because it divides x3 + 1 exactly, a double-bit error with the two errors 3 positions apart goes undetected (P3 fails). Overall CRC is much stronger than checksum, and is well suited to a hardware (shift-register) implementation at the link layer.

25S2 Q2(b)SMC：三个 C 类网络的 FLSM 与 VLSM15 分 · 子网
SMC 拿到 210.21.20.0/24、210.21.21.0/24、210.21.22.0/24。部门：HR 10、Logistics 20、M&S 40、PT1 60、PT2 60、R&D 60；路由器之间链路 A–F 共 6 条。可以用全 0 和全 1 子网。(i) FLSM 设计，连 Internet 的路由器向上游宣告的汇总路由？(ii) VLSM 设计（少浪费、多聚合），最有效的汇总路由？

(ii) VLSM

PT1、PT2、R&D 各 60 → /26（64）；M&S 40 → /26（64）；Logistics 20 → /27（32）；HR 10 → /28（16）；6 条链路 → 6 × /30（24）总共 4×64 + 32 + 16 + 24 = 328 > 256 → 要两个 C 类，选能对齐聚合的 20 和 21

English answer

(i) FLSM: all subnets /26

Allocation table: see above.

Twelve /26 subnets exactly fill the three Class C networks. The third octet of the three networks: 20 = 00010100, 21 = 00010101, 22 = 00010110; the common prefix is 22 bits, so the summary route is 210.21.20.0/22. Note this also covers 210.21.23.0/24, which does not belong to SMC; if that is not allowed, advertise two routes instead: 210.21.20.0/23 and 210.21.22.0/24.

(ii) VLSM

  • PT1, PT2, R&D (60 each) → /26 (64); M&S 40 → /26 (64); Logistics 20 → /27 (32); HR 10 → /28 (16); 6 links → 6 × /30 (24)
  • Total = 4×64 + 32 + 16 + 24 = 328 > 256 → two Class C networks are needed; choose 20 and 21, which can align for aggregation.

Allocation table: see above.

Only 210.21.20.0/24 and 210.21.21.0/24 are used; they align exactly into one /23, giving summary route 210.21.20.0/23. 210.21.22.0/24 is left entirely for future expansion.

2025/26 S1

November / December 2025

Q1：三类接入网、分组大小优化、超网 + 子网 + 扩容。Q2：Checksum + CRC、MAC 曲线、选择重传、分片。

25S1 Q1(a)三类接入网6 分 · 文字
One way to categorize the network architecture is into network edge, access, and core. Briefly explain the three types of access networks.
网络体系结构的一种划分方法是分成三部分：网络边缘、接入网、网络核心。简要说明三类接入网。
参考答案

接入网把端系统连到第一台（边缘）路由器，按使用场景分三类（Topic 1 slide 17–22）：

  • 住宅接入：拨号 modem（用电话网，最高 56 kbps，上网和打电话不能同时）；DSL（用电话线但频段分开：0–4 kHz 电话、4–50 kHz 上行、50 kHz–1 MHz 下行，上行 ≤1 Mbps、下行 ≤8 Mbps，到电话局 DSLAM 是专用线路）；Cable modem（HFC 混合光纤同轴，下行 ≤30 Mbps、上行 ≤2 Mbps，多户共享到 ISP 路由器）；FTTH 光纤到户。
  • 机构接入（学校、公司）：以太网，10 Mbps / 100 Mbps / 1 Gbps / 10 Gbps；端系统接以太网交换机，经机构路由器连 ISP。
  • 移动（无线）接入：共享的无线接入网经基站/接入点把端系统连到路由器。无线 LAN（IEEE 802.11b/g，11/54 Mbps，覆盖一栋楼）和广域无线接入（运营商提供的蜂窝网 GSM、3G、4G/LTE）。

English answer

The access network connects end systems to the first (edge) router. It is divided into three types by use case (Topic 1, slides 17–22):

  • Residential access: dial-up modem (uses the telephone network, up to 56 kbps, cannot browse and talk on the phone at the same time); DSL (uses the phone line but splits the frequency band: 0–4 kHz for voice, 4–50 kHz upstream, 50 kHz–1 MHz downstream; upstream ≤ 1 Mbps, downstream ≤ 8 Mbps, with a dedicated line to the telco's DSLAM); cable modem (HFC, hybrid fiber-coax, downstream ≤ 30 Mbps, upstream ≤ 2 Mbps, shared by multiple households up to the ISP's router); FTTH (fiber to the home).
  • Institutional access (schools, companies): Ethernet, at 10 Mbps / 100 Mbps / 1 Gbps / 10 Gbps; end systems connect to an Ethernet switch, which connects through the institution's router to the ISP.
  • Mobile (wireless) access: a shared wireless access network connects end systems to a router through a base station or access point. This includes wireless LAN (IEEE 802.11b/g, 11/54 Mbps, covers a building) and wide-area wireless access (cellular networks provided by carriers: GSM, 3G, 4G/LTE).
25S1 Q1(b)存储转发总时延与最优分组大小7 分 · 推导
M bit 报文经 H 跳存储转发网络，每个分组 P bit 载荷 + O bit 开销，M 是 P 的倍数且 M ≫ P，每条链路 C bps，忽略传播、处理、排队。(i) 端到端总时延；(ii) 使总时延最小的 P。
附加练习：M = 106、O = 400、H = 5 时 P* 是多少？
提示

分组数 N = M/P，每个分组传输时间 (P + O)/C，套 (L + N − 1)T。展开后对 P 求导。

完整解答
(i) N = M/P，T = (P + O)/CD = (H + M/P − 1)(P + O) / C(ii) 展开：D = [M + MO/P + (H − 1)P + (H − 1)O] / CdD/dP = [−MO/P² + (H − 1)] / C = 0P* = √( MO / (H − 1) )（二阶导 2MO/(P³C) > 0，是最小值）附加练习：√(106 × 400 / 4) = √108 = 10 000 bit

直观理解：P 太大，第一个分组要在每一跳都完整传一遍，流水线效应弱；P 太小，分组数多，开销 O 的总量大。H = 1（只有一跳）时没有流水线，P 越大越好。

English answer

  • (i) N = M/P, T = (P + O)/C
  • D = (H + M/P − 1)(P + O) / C
  • (ii) Expand: D = [M + MO/P + (H − 1)P + (H − 1)O] / C
  • dD/dP = [−MO/P2 + (H − 1)] / C = 0
  • P* = √( MO / (H − 1) ) (second derivative 2MO/(P3C) > 0, so this is a minimum)
  • Numeric example: √(106 × 400 / 4) = √108 = 10 000 bit

Intuition: if P is too large, the first packet must be fully transmitted on every hop in turn, so the pipelining effect is weak; if P is too small, there are many packets and the total overhead O adds up. When H = 1 (a single hop) there is no pipelining benefit, so a larger P is always better.

25S1 Q2(a)Checksum 与 CRC（x³ + x² + x + 1）8 分
12 位数据 111011000011。(i) 4 位块长的 checksum；(ii) C(x) = x³ + x² + x + 1 时的 CRC 码字，并验证这个 C(x) 能检测所有奇数个错误。
提示

1110 = 14，1100 = 12，0011 = 3。C(x) 写成比特是 1111。验证：看 C(x) 有没有 (x + 1) 因子。

完整解答
(i) 14 + 12 + 3 = 29；29 mod 15 = 14；checksum = 15 − 14 = 1 = 0001(ii) 111011000011 000 ÷ 1111 → 余数 111 → 码字 111011000011 111验证：C(1) = 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 0 (mod 2)，所以 (x + 1) 是因子长除法：(x³ + x² + x + 1) ÷ (x + 1) = x² + 1，即 C(x) = (x + 1)(x² + 1)

奇数个比特错误的 E(x) 有奇数项，E(1) = 1 ≠ 0，所以 (x + 1) 不整除 E(x)，C(x) 也就不整除 E(x) → 一定能检测（性质 P2）。

English answer

  • (i) 14 + 12 + 3 = 29; 29 mod 15 = 14; checksum = 15 − 14 = 1 = 0001
  • (ii) 111011000011 000 ÷ 1111 → remainder 111 → codeword 111011000011 111
  • Verification: C(1) = 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 0 (mod 2), so (x + 1) is a factor
  • Long division: (x3 + x2 + x + 1) ÷ (x + 1) = x2 + 1, i.e. C(x) = (x + 1)(x2 + 1)

An error pattern E(x) with an odd number of bit errors has an odd number of terms, so E(1) = 1 ≠ 0; therefore (x + 1) does not divide E(x), and so C(x) cannot divide E(x) either → it is always detected (property P2).

25S1 Q2(b)比较随机接入协议的 Smax–a 曲线5 分 · 文字
Figure 2（即 Topic 2.2 slide 31）给出 CSMA/CD、1-P CSMA、Non-P CSMA、Slotted ALOHA、ALOHA 的最大吞吐量随 a 的变化。分析曲线并比较性能。
参考答案
  • ALOHA 和 Slotted ALOHA 是水平线（0.184 和 0.368）：它们不侦听信道，性能和 a 无关。
  • CSMA 一族都随 a 增大而下降：侦听到的是 tprop 之前的信道状态，a 越大，"听到空闲其实别人已经开始发"的概率越大，脆弱期 tprop 相对帧长越长。
  • a 小（0.01）时 CSMA/CD 最好（接近 1），其次 Non-P CSMA（约 0.8），再次 1-P CSMA（约 0.5）。CSMA/CD 发现碰撞就中止，浪费最少；非坚持比 1-坚持碰撞少。
  • a 增大到 0.1 以上时 CSMA 类快速下降；a 接近 1 时 CSMA 类降到 0.2 以下，反而不如 Slotted ALOHA，甚至不如 ALOHA。
  • 结论：局域网（距离短、a 小）适合 CSMA/CD；传播时延相对帧长很大的网络（如卫星、a 大）用 ALOHA 类更好。

English answer

  • ALOHA and Slotted ALOHA are flat lines (0.184 and 0.368): they do not sense the channel, so their performance does not depend on a.
  • The whole CSMA family declines as a increases: what a station senses reflects the channel state before tprop has elapsed; the larger a is, the more likely it is that "the channel looks idle but someone has already started transmitting", because the vulnerable period tprop is larger relative to the frame time.
  • At small a (0.01), CSMA/CD is best (close to 1), followed by non-persistent CSMA (about 0.8), then 1-persistent CSMA (about 0.5). CSMA/CD aborts as soon as a collision is detected, wasting the least; non-persistent CSMA has fewer collisions than 1-persistent.
  • Once a rises above about 0.1 the CSMA family drops rapidly; near a = 1 the CSMA family falls below 0.2, actually worse than Slotted ALOHA, and even worse than plain ALOHA.
  • Conclusion: LANs (short distance, small a) suit CSMA/CD; networks where propagation delay is large relative to frame time (e.g. satellite links, large a) are better served by the ALOHA family.
25S1 Q2(c)选择重传 ARQ 的载荷吞吐量6 分
SR ARQ，窗口 15。A 连续发 500 字节帧（450 字节载荷 + 50 字节开销），B 对每个成功帧回 50 字节 ACK。链路 500 Mbps，5 km，传播 2×105 km/s，BER = 5×10−5。平均载荷吞吐量？
提示

2×105 km/s = 2×108 m/s。先判断 W 和 2a + 1 的大小关系。

完整解答
tf = 4000 / (500×106) = 8 µs；tprop = 5 / (2×105) = 25 µsa = 25 / 8 = 3.125 → 2a + 1 = 7.25（计入 ACK 0.8 µs 后为 58.8/8 = 7.35）W = 15 ≥ 2a + 1 → 窗口不会用完，U = 1 − pp = 1 − (1 − 5×10−5)4000 = 0.1813 → U = 0.8187载荷吞吐量 = 0.8187 × 500 Mbps × 450/500 = 368.4 Mbps

这里计不计 ACK 结果一样，因为两种情况都是 W ≥ 2a + 1。

English answer

  • tf = 4000 / (500×106) = 8 µs; tprop = 5 / (2×105) = 25 µs
  • a = 25 / 8 = 3.125 → 2a + 1 = 7.25 (including the ACK time of 0.8 µs this becomes 58.8/8 = 7.35)
  • W = 15 ≥ 2a + 1 → the window never runs out, so U = 1 − p
  • p = 1 − (1 − 5×10−5)4000 = 0.1813 → U = 0.8187
  • Payload throughput = 0.8187 × 500 Mbps × 450/500 = 368.4 Mbps

Whether or not the ACK time is included gives the same result here, because both cases satisfy W ≥ 2a + 1.

24S2 Q1(a)DHCP 的运作5 分 · 文字
In your own words, describe the operation of Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCP).
用你自己的话，描述动态主机配置协议（DHCP）是怎么工作的。
参考答案

DHCP 让主机加入网络时从服务器动态获得 IP 地址（"即插即用"），地址有租期，可以续租，断开后可以给别人复用，方便移动用户。过程四步，因为主机还没有地址，四条消息都用广播（服务器端口 67，客户端端口 68）：

  1. DHCP discover：主机以源 0.0.0.0、目的 255.255.255.255 广播，寻找 DHCP 服务器。
  2. DHCP offer：服务器回应，提供一个可用地址（yiaddr）和租期（如 3600 s）。
  3. DHCP request：主机请求使用这个地址（也是广播，让其他服务器知道它选了谁）。
  4. DHCP ACK：服务器确认，地址正式分配。

除 IP 地址外，DHCP 还能告诉主机：默认网关（第一跳路由器）地址、DNS 服务器名字和地址、子网掩码。前两步是可选的（主机可以直接 request 以前用过的地址）。

English answer

DHCP lets a host dynamically obtain an IP address from a server when it joins a network ("plug-and-play"). The address has a lease time, can be renewed, and can be reused by another host after it disconnects, which is convenient for mobile users. The process has four steps; since the host has no address yet, all four messages are broadcast (server port 67, client port 68):

  1. DHCP discover: the host broadcasts with source 0.0.0.0 and destination 255.255.255.255, looking for a DHCP server.
  2. DHCP offer: a server replies, offering an available address (yiaddr) and a lease time (e.g. 3600 s).
  3. DHCP request: the host requests that address (also broadcast, so other servers know which offer was chosen).
  4. DHCP ACK: the server confirms, and the address is formally assigned.

Besides the IP address, DHCP can also tell the host: the default gateway (first-hop router) address, the DNS server's name and address, and the subnet mask. The first two steps are optional (a host can go straight to request if it wants to reuse an address it held before).

  • Data = 4000 − 20 = 3980; maximum data per fragment = 1200 − 20 = 1180 → round down to a multiple of 8: 1176
  • 3980 = 1176 × 3 + 452 → 4 fragments

Fragmentation table: see above.

Common mistake: putting 1180 bytes of data in each fragment. 1180 is not a multiple of 8, so the next fragment's offset 1180/8 = 147.5 would not be an integer — the data size must first be rounded down to 1176.

Link G2200.50.6.248/30255.255.255.252200.50.6.249 – 200.50.6.250200.50.6.251
Link H2200.50.6.252/30255.255.255.252200.50.6.253 – 200.50.6.254200.50.6.255
4 = 00000100，5 = 00000101，6 = 00000110 → 公共前缀 22 位汇总路由 200.50.4.0/22（也包含了不属于 ABC 的 200.50.7.0/24；精确宣告则是 200.50.4.0/23 + 200.50.6.0/24）

English answer

  • Engineering 220 → /24; R&D 120, PD 100, Support 80 → /25; Sales 40 → /26; HR 20 → /27; 8 links → /30
  • Total = 256 + 3×128 + 64 + 32 + 8×4 = 768 = 3 × 256, exactly filling all the space.

Allocation table: see above.

  • 4 = 00000100, 5 = 00000101, 6 = 00000110 → common prefix is 22 bits
  • Summary route 200.50.4.0/22 (this also covers 200.50.7.0/24, which does not belong to ABC; a more precise announcement would be 200.50.4.0/23 + 200.50.6.0/24).
24S2 Q2(a)奇偶校验、Checksum、CRC（x³ + x² + 1）11 分
A 发送 12 位数据 1101 1101 0001。(i) 6 位块长的一维偶校验码字；(ii) 4 位块长的 checksum；(iii) g(x) = x³ + x² + 1 的 CRC 码字。若收到的校验位全错，B 的检错过程和结果？
完整解答
(i) 110111：5 个 1 → 校验位 1 → 1101111；010001：2 个 1 → 0 → 0100010    发送 1101111 0100010(ii) 1101 = 13，1101 = 13，0001 = 1；和 27，27 mod 15 = 12 → checksum = 3 = 0011(iii) 110111010001 000 ÷ 1101 → 余数 101 → 码字 110111010001 101    校验位全错：收到 110111010001 010    B 用 1101 去除，余数 = 111 ≠ 0 → 检测到错误

原因：错误多项式 E(x) = x² + x + 1（三位突发，长度 3），g(x) 是 3 次，按性质 P4 所有长度 ≤ 3 的突发都能检测。余数正好就是 E(x) mod g(x) = x² + x + 1 = 111。

English answer

  • (i) 110111: five 1s → parity bit 1 → 1101111; 010001: two 1s → parity bit 0 → 0100010
  • Transmitted codeword: 1101111 0100010
  • (ii) 1101 = 13, 1101 = 13, 0001 = 1; sum = 27, 27 mod 15 = 12 → checksum = 3 = 0011
  • (iii) 110111010001 000 ÷ 1101 → remainder 101 → codeword 110111010001 101
  • With all three check bits flipped, the received word is 110111010001 010
  • B divides by 1101: remainder = 111 ≠ 0 → error detected

Reason: the error polynomial is E(x) = x2 + x + 1 (a 3-bit burst, length 3); g(x) has degree 3, so by property P4 it detects every burst of length ≤ 3. The remainder is exactly E(x) mod g(x) = x2 + x + 1 = 111.

24S2 Q2(b)四台交换机的生成树8 分 · STP
列出 STP 算法 4 步，并为 Figure 2 的以太网求生成树。交换机名字即 ID，端口号标在连线旁，每经过一个 LAN 网段代价 1。拓扑：Switch1 端口 1 接 PC2、端口 2 接 Switch3 端口 4、端口 3 接 Switch2 端口 1、端口 4 接 Switch4 端口 1；Switch2 端口 2 接 Switch3 端口 3、端口 3 接 PC3、端口 4 接 Switch4 端口 2；Switch3 端口 1 接 PC4、端口 2 接 PC1；Switch4 端口 3 接 PC5。
完整解答

四步：① 选 ID 最小的网桥为根；② 每个非根网桥选到根代价最小的端口为根端口；③ 每个 LAN 选到根代价最小的网桥为指定网桥（平局选 ID 小的），它在该 LAN 上的端口是指定端口；④ 根端口和指定端口转发，其余端口阻塞。

  • Switch1 ID 最小 → 根交换机，它的所有端口都是指定端口。
  • Switch2、3、4 都直连 Switch1（代价 1）→ 根端口分别是 Switch2 端口 1、Switch3 端口 4、Switch4 端口 1。
  • Switch2–Switch3 网段：两端到根代价都是 1，Switch2 ID 小 → Switch2 端口 2 指定，Switch3 端口 3 阻塞。
  • Switch2–Switch4 网段：同理 Switch2 端口 4 指定，Switch4 端口 2 阻塞。
  • 接 PC 的网段只有一台交换机，它的端口就是指定端口。
Root SwitchRoot Port(s)Designated Port(s)Blocked Port(s)
Switch1Yes1, 2, 3, 4
Switch2No12, 3, 4
Switch3No41, 23
Switch4No132

端口号按题图读出；如果你看的图端口编号不同，按同样的四步重新推一遍即可。

English answer

Four steps: ① elect the bridge with the smallest ID as the root; ② every non-root bridge chooses the port with the lowest cost to the root as its root port; ③ on every LAN, the bridge with the lowest cost to the root becomes the designated bridge (ties broken by the smaller ID), and its port on that LAN is the designated port; ④ root ports and designated ports forward, all other ports are blocked.

  • Switch1 has the smallest ID → root switch; all of its ports are designated ports.
  • Switch2, 3 and 4 are all directly connected to Switch1 (cost 1) → their root ports are Switch2 port 1, Switch3 port 4, Switch4 port 1 respectively.
  • Switch2–Switch3 segment: both ends have cost 1 to the root; Switch2 has the smaller ID → Switch2 port 2 is designated, Switch3 port 3 is blocked.
  • Switch2–Switch4 segment: similarly, Switch2 port 4 is designated, Switch4 port 2 is blocked.
  • A segment with only one switch and some PCs has that switch's port as the designated port by default.

Table: see above.

Port numbers are read off the figure in the question; if your copy of the figure numbers the ports differently, re-derive them with the same four steps.

24S2 Q2(c)滑动窗口最大有效速率6 分
滑动窗口流量控制，600 Mbps 链路，长 50 km，传播 2×108 m/s。数据帧 600 字节，窗口 20，接收方对每个成功帧回 60 字节 ACK。最大有效数据速率？
完整解答
tf = 4800 / (600×106) = 8 µs；tack = 480 / (600×106) = 0.8 µs；tprop = 50 000 / (2×108) = 250 µsa = 250 / 8 = 31.25 → 2a + 1 = 63.5 ≫ W = 20 → 窗口会用完一个周期 = tf + 2tprop + tack = 8 + 500 + 0.8 = 508.8 µs，期间发 20 帧有效速率 = 20 × 4800 / 508.8 µs = 188.68 Mbps（U = 0.3145）

不计 ACK：U = W/(2a+1) = 20/63.5 = 0.315 → 188.98 Mbps。两种都可以，写清楚假设。

English answer

  • tf = 4800 / (600×106) = 8 µs; tack = 480 / (600×106) = 0.8 µs; tprop = 50 000 / (2×108) = 250 µs
  • a = 250 / 8 = 31.25 → 2a + 1 = 63.5 ≫ W = 20 → the window will run out
  • One cycle = tf + 2tprop + tack = 8 + 500 + 0.8 = 508.8 µs, during which 20 frames are sent
  • Effective rate = 20 × 4800 / 508.8 µs = 188.68 Mbps (U = 0.3145)

Without counting the ACK time: U = W/(2a + 1) = 20/63.5 = 0.315 → 188.98 Mbps. Either is acceptable as long as the assumption is stated clearly.

2024/25 S1

November / December 2024

Q1：NAT、Checksum + CRC + 性质分析、时隙长度 + 交换机转发表。Q2：两个 C 类的 FLSM/VLSM + 汇总、带处理时延的存储转发公式。

24S1 Q1(a)NAT 的好处与争议5 分 · 文字
What are the advantages and controversial points about implementing the Network Address Translation (NAT) protocol?
使用网络地址转换（NAT）协议有哪些优点？又有哪些有争议的地方？
参考答案

优点：本地网对外只需一个 IP 地址，不用向 ISP 申请一段地址；改内网地址不用通知外界；换 ISP 不用改内网地址；内网设备不能被外界直接寻址，安全上有好处；16 位端口号让一个外部地址能支持约 6 万个同时连接。

争议：路由器本应只处理到第 3 层，NAT 却要改第 4 层端口；地址短缺本应由 IPv6 解决；违反端到端原则，应用设计者必须考虑 NAT（如 P2P）；外部客户端很难连到 NAT 后面的服务器（NAT 穿越问题）。

English answer

Advantages: the local network needs only one public IP address and does not need to request a block of addresses from the ISP; internal addresses can be changed without notifying the outside world; changing ISP does not require renumbering the internal network; internal devices cannot be addressed directly from outside, which helps security; the 16-bit port number lets a single external address support roughly 60 000 simultaneous connections.

Controversial points: a router is supposed to process only up to layer 3, but NAT has to rewrite layer-4 port numbers; the address shortage was supposed to be solved by IPv6; it violates the end-to-end principle, forcing application designers to work around NAT (e.g. for P2P); it is hard for an external client to connect to a server sitting behind NAT (the NAT traversal problem).

24S1 Q1(b)8 位 Checksum、CRC（x⁴+x²+x+1）与性质分析10 分
T 发送 16 位数据 10100001 00011000。(i) 8 位块长 checksum；(ii) C(x) = x⁴ + x² + x + 1 的 CRC 码字；若 R 收到的码字左数第 2 位错了，R 怎么发现？(iii) 用至少 3 条 CRC 性质分析这个生成多项式。
完整解答
(i) 10100001 = 161，00011000 = 24；和 185 < 255；checksum = 255 − 185 = 70 = 01000110(ii) C = 10111（4 次），补 4 个 0：1010000100011000 0000 ÷ 10111 → 余数 1011    码字 10100001 00011000 1011（20 位）    第 2 位错：收到 11100001000110001011，除以 10111 余 0111 ≠ 0 → 检测到错误

(iii) 性质分析

  • P1：C(x) 有 4 项（多于一项）→ 所有单比特错误都能检测（本题就是单比特错误）。
  • P2：项数为偶数，C(1) = 0 → 含 (x + 1)：C(x) = (x + 1)(x³ + x² + 1) → 所有奇数个错误都能检测。
  • P3：x³ + x² + 1 的周期是 7，C(x) 整除 x⁷ + 1。码字长 n = 20 > 7，所以间隔为 7 的双比特错误（如 xi+7 + xi）检测不到，不能保证检测所有双比特错误。
  • P4：C(x) 是 4 次 → 所有长度 ≤ 4 的突发错误都能检测。

English answer

  • (i) 10100001 = 161, 00011000 = 24; sum = 185 < 255; checksum = 255 − 185 = 70 = 01000110
  • (ii) C = 10111 (degree 4); append 4 zeros: 1010000100011000 0000 ÷ 10111 → remainder 1011
  • Codeword: 10100001 00011000 1011 (20 bits)
  • With bit 2 flipped, the received word is 11100001000110001011; dividing by 10111 gives remainder 0111 ≠ 0 → error detected

(iii) Property analysis

  • P1: C(x) has 4 terms (more than one) → it detects all single-bit errors (which is exactly the error in this question).
  • P2: the number of terms is even and C(1) = 0 → it contains the factor (x + 1): C(x) = (x + 1)(x3 + x2 + 1) → it detects all errors with an odd number of bits.
  • P3: x3 + x2 + 1 has period 7, so C(x) divides x7 + 1. The codeword length n = 20 > 7, so a double-bit error 7 positions apart (e.g. xi+7 + xi) is not detected — C(x) cannot guarantee detection of all double-bit errors.
  • P4: C(x) has degree 4 → it detects all burst errors of length ≤ 4.
24S1 Q1(c)时隙长度、交换机转发表、两个同时的传输10 分
拓扑见学习手册的交换机自学习实验（SW1 端口 1/2 接 PC1/PC2，端口 3 接 SW2，端口 4 接 SW3；SW2 端口 2 接 Hub1（PC3、PC4）；SW3 端口 2 接 Hub2（PC7，以及分别经一个集线器接的 PC5、PC6））。每段链路 100 m、100 Mbps、2×108 m/s，帧 1518 字节，交换机是二层设备，集线器是一层设备。(i) Slotted ALOHA 和 CSMA/CD 的时隙长度；(ii) 所有设备都发过帧后 SW1、SW2、SW3 的转发表；(iii) PC3 → PC2 和 PC6 → PC7 同时发生时三台交换机怎么做？
提示

时隙 ALOHA 的时隙 = 一帧的传输时间。CSMA/CD 的时隙 = 2tprop，tprop 按同一个碰撞域里最远两点的距离算：交换机隔开碰撞域，集线器不隔开。

完整解答

(i)

Slotted ALOHA：时隙 = F = 1518 × 8 / 108 = 121.44 µsCSMA/CD：最大的碰撞域是 SW3 端口 2 以下：PC5–Hub–Hub2–Hub–PC6，共 4 段 = 400 mtprop = 400 / (2×108) = 2 µs → 时隙 2tprop = 4 µs

若按单段链路 100 m 算，2tprop = 1 µs。按碰撞域最远距离算才能保证所有碰撞都被检测到，建议写 4 µs 并说明理由。其他碰撞域：SW2 下 PC3–Hub1–PC4 为 200 m（2 µs）；交换机之间、交换机到 PC1/PC2 各 100 m（1 µs）。

(ii) 转发表（PC → 端口）

PC1PC2PC3PC4PC5PC6PC7
SW11233444
SW21122111
SW31111222

(iii)

  • PC3 → PC2：Hub1 把帧送到 PC4 和 SW2 端口 2。SW2 查表 PC2 在端口 1 → 转发到端口 1。SW1 从端口 3 收到，查表 PC2 在端口 2 → 转发给 PC2。SW3 收不到这一帧。
  • PC6 → PC7：PC6 的集线器 → Hub2 → 广播到 PC7、PC5 那一路和 SW3 端口 2。PC7 直接收到。SW3 查表 PC7 在端口 2，和进入端口相同 → 过滤（丢弃），不往 SW1 转发。
  • 两个传输在不同的碰撞域里，可以同时进行，不会碰撞。SW1 只参与第一个，SW2 只参与第一个，SW3 只在第二个里做过滤。

English answer

(i)

  • Slotted ALOHA: slot = F = 1518 × 8 / 108 = 121.44 µs
  • CSMA/CD: the largest collision domain is below SW3 port 2: PC5–Hub–Hub2–Hub–PC6, 4 segments = 400 m
  • tprop = 400 / (2×108) = 2 µs → slot = 2tprop = 4 µs

If computed using a single 100 m segment, 2tprop = 1 µs. Using the farthest distance within the collision domain is the only way to guarantee every collision is detected, so 4 µs with this reasoning is the recommended answer. Other collision domains: under SW2, PC3–Hub1–PC4 is 200 m (2 µs); between switches, and from a switch to PC1/PC2, each is 100 m (1 µs).

(ii) Forwarding tables (PC → port)

Table: see above.

(iii)

  • PC3 → PC2: Hub1 delivers the frame to PC4 and to SW2 port 2. SW2 looks up PC2, which is on port 1 → forwards out port 1. SW1 receives it on port 3, looks up PC2 on port 2 → forwards to PC2. SW3 never sees this frame.
  • PC6 → PC7: PC6's hub → Hub2 → broadcasts to the branch with PC7 and PC5, and to SW3 port 2. PC7 receives it directly. SW3 looks up PC7, which is on port 2 — the same port the frame arrived on — so it filters (drops) the frame and does not forward it to SW1.
  • The two transmissions are in different collision domains, so they can happen at the same time without colliding. SW1 is involved only in the first one, SW2 only in the first one, and SW3 only filters in the second one.
24S1 Q2(a)SDC：两个不连续 C 类的 FLSM 与 VLSM16 分 · 子网
198.2.45.0/24 和 198.2.46.0/24。HR 5、Logistics 16、M&S 12、Product Design 26、Engineering 30；链路 A–G 共 7 条。(i) FLSM，不用全 0/全 1 子网，汇总路由？(ii) VLSM，可以用全 0/全 1，少浪费多聚合，最有效的汇总路由？(iii) 上游路由器还连着 198.2.47.0/24、198.2.48.0/22、198.2.52.0/24、198.2.53.0/24、198.2.54.0/24，它向更高层 ISP 宣告的汇总路由？

English answer

(i) FLSM: /27, 6 usable per Class C network

Allocation table: see above.

45 = 00101101, 46 = 00101110, common prefix 16 + 6 = 22 bits → 198.2.44.0/22 (45 and 46 cannot align into a /23, so this summary route also covers 44 and 47).

(ii) VLSM: everything fits into one Class C network

  • Engineering 30, PD 26, Logistics 16 → /27; M&S 12 → /28; HR 5 → /29; 7 links → /30
  • Total = 3×32 + 16 + 8 + 28 = 148 ≤ 256 → only 198.2.45.0/24 is used

Allocation table: see above.

Using only one Class C network, the summary route is simply 198.2.45.0/24; 198.2.46.0/24 is left entirely unused.

(iii)

  • Third octet 45 to 54: 45 = 0010 1101, 54 = 0011 0110, common prefix is only the first 3 bits, 001
  • Prefix = 16 + 3 = 19 → 198.2.32.0/19 (covers 198.2.32.0 to 198.2.63.255)

A single summary route covers many networks that do not belong to these sites. If multiple routes may be advertised, a tighter pair is 198.2.44.0/22 (44–47) + 198.2.48.0/21 (48–55).

24S1 Q2(b)S 台交换机的存储转发总时间9 分 · 推导
Adam 经 S 台存储转发交换机给 Betty 发 M 字节报文，每台交换机处理每个分组 P 秒。每段链路长 D 米、速率 C bps、传播速度 V m/s。每个分组 H 字节首部 + L 字节载荷，M 是 L 的倍数，节点可以不等确认连续发。忽略 Adam/Betty 的处理和所有排队，推导总时间。
附加练习：M = 8000，L = 1000，H = 80，S = 2，C = 1 Mbps，D/V = 10 ms，P = 1 ms 时的总时间（ms）？
完整解答
分组数 N = M/L；S 台交换机 → S + 1 段链路每个分组的传输时间 T = 8(L + H)/C第一个分组到达 Betty：(S + 1)·T + (S + 1)·D/V + S·P其余 N − 1 个分组每隔 T 到一个：(M/L − 1)·T总时间 = (S + M/L) · 8(L + H)/C + (S + 1) · D/V + S · P附加练习：(2 + 8) × 8.64 + 3 × 10 + 2 × 1 = 86.4 + 32 = 118.4 ms（就是 Session 2 扩展例题 (b)）

假设处理时延不超过一个分组的传输时间（P ≤ T），这样处理可以流水进行，后续分组仍每隔 T 到达。

English answer

  • Number of packets N = M/L; S switches → S + 1 links
  • Transmission time of one packet: T = 8(L + H)/C
  • Time for the first packet to reach Betty: (S + 1)·T + (S + 1)·D/V + S·P
  • The remaining N − 1 packets each arrive one T apart: (M/L − 1)·T
  • Total time = (S + M/L) · 8(L + H)/C + (S + 1) · D/V + S · P
  • Numeric example: (2 + 8) × 8.64 + 3 × 10 + 2 × 1 = 86.4 + 32 = 118.4 ms (this is the Session 2 extended worked example (b)).

This assumes the processing delay never exceeds one packet's transmission time (P ≤ T), so processing can be pipelined and later packets still arrive one T apart.

2023/24 S1

November / December 2023

Q1：三种协议的脆弱期、Checksum + CRC 的检错比较、选择重传。Q2：CSMA/CD vs CSMA/CA、ARP vs DHCP、一个 C 类的子网 + 扩容 + 汇总。

23S1 Q1(a)ALOHA、时隙 ALOHA、CSMA 的脆弱期6 分
拓扑：Hub2 — Hub1 — Switch0 — Switch1 — Hub0。PC1、PC2 接 Hub2；PC0 接 Hub1；PC3 接 Switch0；PC5 接 Switch1；PC4、PC6、PC7 接 Hub0。每段 100 m，传播 2×108 m/s，帧 1500 字节，每段 10 Mbps。分别求 ALOHA、Slotted ALOHA、CSMA 的脆弱期。
完整解答
F = 1500 × 8 / 107 = 1.2 msALOHA：2F = 2.4 ms；Slotted ALOHA：F = 1.2 msCSMA：tprop，按碰撞域内最远两点：    {PC1, PC2, Hub2, Hub1, PC0, Switch0 端口}：PC1–Hub2–Hub1–PC0 = 300 m → 1.5 µs    {Hub0, PC4, PC6, PC7, Switch1 端口}：200 m → 1 µs    Switch0–PC3、Switch0–Switch1、Switch1–PC5：100 m → 0.5 µs

交换机把网络分成了 5 个碰撞域；集线器把它连接的所有网段并成一个碰撞域。

English answer

  • F = 1500 × 8 / 107 = 1.2 ms
  • ALOHA: 2F = 2.4 ms; Slotted ALOHA: F = 1.2 ms
  • CSMA: tprop is taken over the two farthest points within a collision domain:
  • {PC1, PC2, Hub2, Hub1, PC0, SW0 port}: PC1–Hub2–Hub1–PC0 = 300 m → 1.5 µs
  • {Hub0, PC4, PC6, PC7, SW1 port}: 200 m → 1 µs
  • SW0–PC3, SW0–SW1, SW1–PC5: 100 m → 0.5 µs

The switches split the network into 5 collision domains; a hub merges every segment attached to it into a single collision domain.

23S1 Q1(b)第 3、6 位出错时 Checksum 和 CRC 能否发现12 分
A 发送 12 位数据 1110 1000 0001。(i) 4 位块长 checksum 的码字；若码字左数第 3 位和第 6 位错了，R 的结果？(ii) C(x) = x³ + x² + 1 的 CRC 码字；同样第 3、6 位错，R 的结果？(iii) 比较两种方法。
完整解答

(i) Checksum

14 + 8 + 1 = 23；23 mod 15 = 8；checksum = 7 = 0111码字 1110 1000 0001 0111第 3、6 位翻转：1100 1100 0001 0111 → 12 + 12 + 1 + 7 = 3232 mod 15 = 2 ≠ 0 → 检测到错误

(ii) CRC

1110 1000 0001 000 ÷ 1101 → 余数 100 → 码字 111010000001 100第 3、6 位翻转：110011000001100，除以 1101 余 111 ≠ 0 → 检测到错误E(x) = x¹² + x⁹ = x⁹(x³ + 1)，x³ + x² + 1 不整除 x³ + 1 → 能检测

(iii)

这次两种方法都发现了错误。但 checksum 能发现只是因为两处错误一个让第一个字 −2、一个让第二个字 +4，没有抵消；如果错误正好让一个字 +k、另一个字 −k，checksum 就检测不到。CRC 看的是错误的位置模式，C(x) = x³ + x² + 1 是 3 次本原多项式，周期 7：能检测所有单比特错误、所有长度 ≤ 3 的突发、所有间隔小于 7 的双比特错误；它没有 (x+1) 因子，不保证检测所有奇数个错误。总体上 CRC 检错能力更强，checksum 实现更简单。

English answer

(i) Checksum

  • 14 + 8 + 1 = 23; 23 mod 15 = 8; checksum = 7 = 0111
  • Codeword: 1110 1000 0001 0111
  • With bits 3 and 6 flipped: 1100 1100 0001 0111 → 12 + 12 + 1 + 7 = 32
  • 32 mod 15 = 2 ≠ 0 → error detected

(ii) CRC

  • 1110 1000 0001 000 ÷ 1101 → remainder 100 → codeword 111010000001 100
  • With bits 3 and 6 flipped: 110011000001100, divided by 1101 leaves remainder 111 ≠ 0 → error detected
  • E(x) = x12 + x9 = x9(x3 + 1); x3 + x2 + 1 does not divide x3 + 1 → detectable

(iii)

This time both methods caught the error. But checksum only succeeded because the two bit errors changed one word by −2 and the other by +4, which did not cancel out; if the errors had instead been exactly +k in one word and −k in the other, checksum would have missed it. CRC instead looks at the position pattern of the errors: C(x) = x3 + x2 + 1 is a degree-3 primitive polynomial with period 7, so it detects all single-bit errors, all bursts of length ≤ 3, and all double-bit errors less than 7 positions apart; it has no (x + 1) factor, so it does not guarantee detecting all odd numbers of errors. Overall CRC has stronger error-detecting power, while checksum is simpler to implement.

23S1 Q1(c)选择重传 W = 7 的载荷吞吐量7 分
SR ARQ，窗口 7。链路 1 Gbps，5 km，传播 2×105 km/s，BER = 3×10−5。数据帧 480 字节（400 字节载荷 + 80 字节开销），R 对每个成功帧回 80 字节 ACK。平均载荷吞吐量？
计入 ACK 时间得 366.4，不计得 370.8，两者都算对。
完整解答
tf = 3840 / 109 = 3.84 µs；tack = 640 / 109 = 0.64 µs；tprop = 25 µsa = 25 / 3.84 = 6.51 → 2a + 1 = 14.02 > W = 7 → 窗口会用完p = 1 − (1 − 3×10−5)3840 = 0.1088讲义公式：U = W(1 − p)/(2a + 1) = 7 × 0.8912 / 14.02 = 0.4449载荷吞吐量 = 0.4449 × 1 Gbps × 400/480 = 370.8 Mbps计入 ACK：周期 = 3.84 + 50 + 0.64 = 54.48 µs，U = 7 × 3.84 × 0.8912 / 54.48 = 0.4397 → 366.4 Mbps

English answer

  • tf = 3840 / 109 = 3.84 µs; tack = 640 / 109 = 0.64 µs; tprop = 25 µs
  • a = 25 / 3.84 = 6.51 → 2a + 1 = 14.02 > W = 7 → the window will run out
  • p = 1 − (1 − 3×10−5)3840 = 0.1088
  • Using the course formula: U = W(1 − p)/(2a + 1) = 7 × 0.8912 / 14.02 = 0.4449
  • Payload throughput = 0.4449 × 1 Gbps × 400/480 = 370.8 Mbps
  • Including the ACK time: cycle = 3.84 + 50 + 0.64 = 54.48 µs, U = 7 × 3.84 × 0.8912 / 54.48 = 0.4397 → 366.4 Mbps
23S1 Q2(a)CSMA/CD vs CSMA/CA5 分 · 文字
Compare and contrast CSMA/CD and CSMA/CA, and the types of networks in which they are implemented.
比较 CSMA/CD 和 CSMA/CA 的相同点和不同点，并说明它们各自用在什么类型的网络里。
参考答案

相同：都是随机接入，都先侦听信道（CSMA），信道忙就推迟，碰撞后随机退避。

CSMA/CD 用在有线以太网（IEEE 802.3，共享总线或集线器）：边发边听，检测到碰撞立刻中止并发 32 bit jam 信号，然后截断二进制指数退避；最多 2tprop 发现碰撞，浪费小，a 小时效率 1/(1 + 6.44a) 很高。没有 ACK。

CSMA/CA 用在无线局域网（IEEE 802.11 a/b/g/n）：无线上发送时自己信号太强，难以同时接收；隐藏终端和衰落也让发送方察觉不到接收方处的碰撞，所以碰撞检测不可行。改为避免碰撞：发送方先发短的 RTS，AP 广播 CTS，所有站（包括隐藏站）听到 CTS 后推迟，信道为发送方预留，再发数据帧。碰撞只会发生在很短的 RTS 上，解决了隐藏节点问题。

English answer

Similarities: both are random-access protocols, both sense the channel first (CSMA), both defer when the channel is busy, and both back off randomly after a collision.

CSMA/CD is used on wired Ethernet (IEEE 802.3, shared bus or hub): it listens while transmitting, and as soon as a collision is detected it aborts immediately and sends a 32-bit jam signal, followed by truncated binary exponential backoff; a collision is found within at most 2tprop, so little is wasted, and efficiency 1/(1 + 6.44a) is high when a is small. There is no ACK.

CSMA/CA is used on wireless LANs (IEEE 802.11 a/b/g/n): while a station is transmitting, its own signal is too strong for it to also receive, and hidden terminals and fading mean the sender cannot sense a collision happening at the receiver, so collision detection is not feasible. Instead it avoids collisions: the sender first sends a short RTS, the AP broadcasts a CTS, and every station (including hidden ones) that hears the CTS defers, reserving the channel for the sender before the data frame is sent. Collisions can then only happen on the short RTS, which solves the hidden-terminal problem.

23S1 Q2(b)ARP 与 DHCP7 分 · 文字
Explain ARP and DHCP. Briefly compare and contrast these two protocols.
解释 ARP 和 DHCP，并简要比较这两个协议的异同。
参考答案

DHCP：主机加入网络时动态获取自己的 IP 地址（及子网掩码、默认网关、DNS）。四步广播：discover → offer → request → ACK；客户端端口 68，服务器端口 67；地址有租期，可续租、可复用。

ARP（讲义只在 Topic 3 slide 16 提到"用于地址映射"）：已知同一子网内目标的 IP 地址，求它的 MAC 地址，以便封装链路层帧。发送方广播 ARP 请求"谁有 IP x？"，拥有该 IP 的主机单播回复自己的 MAC，发送方存入 ARP 表（有过期时间）。

比较：都在本地网内用广播开始；DHCP 解决"我自己的 IP 是什么"，是客户/服务器模式，需要 DHCP 服务器，跑在 UDP 上；ARP 解决"对方 IP 对应哪个 MAC"，主机之间直接问答，不需要服务器，只在一个广播域（子网）内有效。DHCP 在主机上线时用一次（加续租），ARP 在每次要给新的本地 IP 发帧时都可能用到。

English answer

DHCP: lets a host dynamically obtain its own IP address (plus subnet mask, default gateway, DNS) when it joins a network. Four broadcast steps: discover → offer → request → ACK; client port 68, server port 67; the address has a lease, which can be renewed or reused.

ARP (the notes mention it only in Topic 3, slide 16, as being "used for address mapping"): given the IP address of a destination on the same subnet, it finds that destination's MAC address so a link-layer frame can be built. The sender broadcasts an ARP request, "who has IP x?"; the host owning that IP unicasts back its MAC address, and the sender caches it in its ARP table (which has an expiry time).

Comparison: both start with a broadcast on the local network. DHCP answers "what is my own IP address?" — it is client/server, needs a DHCP server, and runs over UDP. ARP answers "which MAC does that IP belong to?" — hosts ask each other directly, no server is needed, and it only works within one broadcast domain (subnet). DHCP is used once when a host comes online (plus renewals), while ARP may be used every time a frame needs to be sent to a new local IP address.

23S1 Q2(c)英国公司新加坡分部：一个 C 类的子网13 分 · 子网
220.128.20.0/24。CS 10、HR 5、Manufacture 50、M&S 20、PD 40；链路 A–F 共 6 条。(i) 不用全 0/全 1 子网的方案；(ii) 新增 R&D 要 28 个地址，可以用全 0/全 1 子网，只列变化；(iii) 网关向 ISP 上游路由器宣告的路由？上游还连着 220.128.21.0/24 ～ 220.128.24.0/24 时，它向更高层宣告的汇总路由？

(iii)

  • The gateway advertises 220.128.20.0/24.
  • Upstream: 20 = 0001 0100 … 24 = 0001 1000, common prefix is 4 bits → prefix = 16 + 4 = 20
  • Summary route 220.128.16.0/20 (covers 16 through 31)

PART II

网络层 II · 往年 Q3(a)/Q4(a)

CA2 Quiz 只考网络层 II（P2A slides）。下面是 5 份期末卷里属于这一套 slides 的小题，按年份从新到旧排列。其余 Q3/Q4 小题（TCP、可靠传输 FSM、HTTP、P2P、密码学）属于后面几套 slides，等讲义发下来再补。

25S2 Q3(a)不用 Goto 写出 Dijkstra 伪代码5 分
Present the Dijkstra's Algorithm in pseudocodes without any "Goto".
写出 Dijkstra 算法的伪代码，不能用任何 "Goto" 语句。
参考答案（P2A slide 43）
Notation: c(x,y) link cost (∞ if not neighbours); D(v) current cost of path
from source u to v; p(v) predecessor of v; N' set of nodes whose least-cost
path is known.

Initialization:
  N' = {u}
  for all nodes v
    if v adjacent to u then D(v) = c(u,v)  else D(v) = ∞
Loop
  find w not in N' such that D(w) is a minimum
  add w to N'
  update D(v) for all v adjacent to w and not in N':
    D(v) = min( D(v), D(w) + c(w,v) )    /* if improved, p(v) = w */
until all nodes in N'

要点：写清符号含义；用 Loop … until 代替 "Go to step 1"；更新时同时记录前驱 p(v)，最后从 p(·) 回溯得到最短路和转发表。

English answer

Key points (pseudocode above):

  • State the meaning of the notation first: c(x,y), D(v), p(v) and N'.
  • Use a Loop ... until all nodes in N' structure instead of "Go to step 1".
  • When updating D(v), also record the predecessor p(v); at the end, trace back through p(·) to obtain the least-cost paths and the forwarding table.
25S2 Q4(a)SDN 面临的关键挑战5 分
In your own words, briefly elaborate at least two critical challenges faced by software defined networks (SDNs).
用你自己的话，简要阐述软件定义网络（SDN）面临的至少两个关键挑战。
参考答案（P2A slide 119）
  • 加固控制平面：SDN 把"大脑"集中到控制器，控制器一旦故障、过载或被攻击，整个网络都受影响。控制平面必须做成可靠、可依赖、性能可扩展、安全的分布式系统：控制器要有冗余和故障恢复（借用可靠分布式系统的理论），可依赖性和安全性要从设计之初就内置，而不是事后补。
  • 满足特定任务的需求：有些应用要求实时、超高可靠或超高安全，控制器到交换机的往返和集中决策会带来时延，如何保证这些要求是难点。
  • 扩展到 Internet 规模：一个逻辑集中的控制器要维护全网状态、给大量交换机下发流表，规模变大时性能和一致性都很难保证；跨多个管理域的 SDN 也需要域间协调。

English answer

  • Hardening the control plane: SDN centralises the network's "brain" in the controller, so if the controller fails, is overloaded or is attacked, the whole network is affected. The control plane must be a reliable, dependable, performance-scalable and secure distributed system: the controller needs redundancy and failure recovery (drawing on the theory of reliable distributed systems), and dependability and security must be built in from the start of the design, not added afterwards.
  • Meeting mission-specific requirements: some applications require real-time operation, ultra-high reliability or ultra-high security. The controller-to-switch round trip and centralised decision making add delay, so guaranteeing these requirements is difficult.
  • Scaling to Internet scale: a logically centralised controller must maintain the state of the whole network and install flow tables in a large number of switches; as the network grows, performance and consistency become hard to guarantee. SDN spanning multiple administrative domains also needs inter-domain coordination.
25S1 Q3(a)层次 OSPF 怎么工作5 分
In your own words, explain how hierarchical OSPF (Open Shortest Path First) routing works in large networks.
用你自己的话，解释层次化 OSPF 的路由选择在大型网络里是怎么工作的。
参考答案（P2A slide 86–87）
  • OSPF 是链路状态协议：路由器泛洪 LSA，建立相同的链路状态数据库，各自跑 Dijkstra。大网络里全网泛洪和全网计算代价太高，所以把 AS 分成两级层次：若干本地区域（area）和一个主干（backbone）区域。
  • LSA 只在区域内泛洪。区域内的普通路由器只知道本区域的详细拓扑；去别的区域的流量交给本区域的区域边界路由器。
  • 区域边界路由器属于本区域和主干，把到本区域各网络的距离"汇总"，通告给其他区域边界路由器。
  • 主干路由器在主干内运行 OSPF，负责区域之间的转发；边界路由器（AS boundary router）连到其他 AS。
  • 跨区域的分组路径：源区域内部 → 本区域边界路由器 → 主干 → 目的区域边界路由器 → 目的区域内部。
  • 好处：泛洪范围和每台路由器的数据库、计算量都变小，一个区域内的拓扑变化不会引起整个 AS 重新计算，可扩展到大型网络。

English answer

  • OSPF is a link state (LS) protocol: routers flood LSAs, build identical link-state databases and each runs Dijkstra. In a large network, network-wide LSA flooding and network-wide computation are too costly, so the AS is organised as a two-level hierarchy: several local areas and one backbone area.
  • LSAs are flooded only within an area. Internal routers know the detailed topology of their own area only; traffic to other areas is handed to the area's area border router.
  • Area border routers belong to both their own area and the backbone; they "summarise" the distances to the networks in their own area and advertise them to the other area border routers.
  • Backbone routers run OSPF within the backbone and forward traffic between areas; boundary routers (AS boundary routers) connect to other ASes.
  • Path of an inter-area packet: inside the source area → source area border router → backbone → destination area border router → inside the destination area.
  • Benefits: the flooding scope, the size of each router's database and the computation load are all reduced, and a topology change inside one area does not trigger recomputation across the whole AS, so OSPF scales to large networks.
24S2 Q3(a)Bellman-Ford 方程与距离向量算法5 分
State the Bellman-Ford equation and briefly explain how it works in a distance vector routing algorithm.
写出 Bellman-Ford 方程，并简要说明它在距离向量路由算法里是怎么用的。
参考答案（P2A slide 47–52）
Dx(y) = minv { c(x,v) + Dv(y) }，v 取 x 的所有邻居

含义：如果 x 的每个邻居 v 都知道自己到 y 的最短距离 Dv(y)，x 只要算"到邻居的代价 + 邻居到 y 的距离"，取最小值，就得到自己到 y 的最短距离，取到最小值的那个邻居就是下一跳。

在距离向量算法中：每个节点维护到所有目的地的距离向量 DV；初始时到自己为 0、到其他为 ∞；每个节点（定期或在变化时）把自己的 DV 发给邻居；收到邻居的新 DV 后用 Bellman-Ford 方程重算自己的 DV，有变化就再通知邻居。只要节点持续交换，估计值会收敛到真实最小代价。它是分布式、异步、迭代的，只需要邻居之间交换信息。缺点是链路代价升高时可能出现无穷计数问题（坏消息传得慢）。

English answer

Bellman-Ford equation: Dx(y) = minv { c(x,v) + Dv(y) }, where the minimum is taken over all neighbours v of x.

Meaning: if every neighbour v of x knows its own least cost Dv(y) to y, then x only needs to compute "cost to the neighbour + the neighbour's distance to y" for each neighbour and take the minimum. This gives x's least cost to y, and the neighbour that achieves the minimum is the next hop.

Use in the distance vector (DV) algorithm:

  • Each node maintains a distance vector (DV) to all destinations; initially the distance to itself is 0 and to every other node is ∞.
  • Each node sends its DV to its neighbours (periodically or when it changes).
  • When a node receives a new DV from a neighbour, it recomputes its own DV with the Bellman-Ford equation; if its DV changes, it notifies its neighbours again.
  • As long as nodes keep exchanging DVs, the estimates converge to the actual least costs.
  • The algorithm is distributed, asynchronous and iterative, and only requires information exchange between neighbours.
  • Drawback: when a link cost increases, the count-to-infinity problem may occur ("bad news travels slowly").
24S1 Q3(a)LS vs DV：收敛速度与健壮性5 分
Compare link state routing algorithms and distance vector routing algorithms in terms of their (i) convergence speed; and (ii) robustness against malfunctions of a certain router.
从两方面比较链路状态路由算法和距离向量路由算法：(i) 收敛速度；(ii) 某台路由器出故障时的健壮性。
参考答案（P2A slide 63）

(i) 收敛速度：LS 每个节点拿到全网拓扑后用 Dijkstra 一次算完，O(n²) 算法，需要 O(nE) 条消息；收敛快而确定，但在拥塞敏感的路由下可能振荡。DV 的收敛时间不确定，要靠邻居间多轮交换逐步传播；可能出现路由环路，链路代价升高时有无穷计数问题，收敛可能很慢。

(ii) 健壮性：LS 中出故障的路由器可能广播错误的链路代价，但每个节点只根据数据库独立计算自己的表，错误的影响比较局部。DV 中出故障的路由器可能广播错误的路径代价（例如声称到所有地方代价都很小），而每个节点的表会被邻居拿去计算自己的表，错误会在全网传播，所以 LS 更健壮。

English answer

(i) Convergence speed

  • LS: once each node has the complete topology, it computes all routes in one run of Dijkstra, an O(n2) algorithm, with O(nE) messages needed. Convergence is fast and deterministic, but oscillations may occur with congestion-sensitive link costs.
  • DV: convergence time varies; information propagates gradually through many rounds of exchanges between neighbours. Routing loops may occur, and when a link cost increases there is the count-to-infinity problem, so convergence can be very slow.

(ii) Robustness

  • LS: a malfunctioning router may advertise an incorrect link cost, but each node computes only its own table, independently from the database, so the effect of the error is relatively local.
  • DV: a malfunctioning router may advertise incorrect path costs (e.g. claiming a very small cost to every destination), and each node's table is used by its neighbours to compute their own tables, so the error propagates through the whole network.
  • Therefore LS is more robust.
23S1 Q3(a)分布式 Bellman-Ford：到节点 1 的表5 分
图（和 P2A slide 53 同一张图）：1-2 (3)、1-3 (2)、1-4 (5)、2-4 (1)、2-5 (4)、3-4 (2)、3-6 (1)、4-5 (3)、5-6 (2)。求每个节点到目的节点 1 的最小代价和下一跳，填 Iteration 1、2、3 的表（每项为（下一跳，当前最小距离），初始全为 (−1, ∞)）。
提示

每一轮都只用上一轮邻居的值。第 1 轮只有直接连着节点 1 的节点有有限值。可以在学习手册的 Bellman-Ford 实验里选"23/24 S1"预设核对。

完整解答
IterationNode 2Node 3Node 4Node 5Node 6
Initial(−1, ∞)(−1, ∞)(−1, ∞)(−1, ∞)(−1, ∞)
1(1, 3)(1, 2)(1, 5)(−1, ∞)(−1, ∞)
2(1, 3)(1, 2)(2, 4)(2, 7)(3, 3)
3(1, 3)(1, 2)(2, 4)(6, 5)(3, 3)
  • 第 2 轮：节点 4 经 2 是 1 + 3 = 4，经 3 是 2 + 2 = 4，比直连 5 小（两者相同，写 (2,4) 或 (3,4) 都对）；节点 5 经 2 是 4 + 3 = 7，经 4 是 3 + 5 = 8；节点 6 经 3 是 1 + 2 = 3。
  • 第 3 轮：节点 5 经 6 是 2 + 3 = 5，最小。再算一轮没有变化，收敛。

English answer

The table above lists (next hop, current least distance) to destination node 1 for each iteration.

  • Iteration 2: node 4 via node 2 costs 1 + 3 = 4 and via node 3 costs 2 + 2 = 4, both smaller than the direct link cost 5 (a tie, so either (2, 4) or (3, 4) is correct); node 5 via node 2 costs 4 + 3 = 7 and via node 4 costs 3 + 5 = 8; node 6 via node 3 costs 1 + 2 = 3.
  • Iteration 3: node 5 via node 6 costs 2 + 3 = 5, which is the minimum. One more iteration produces no change, so the algorithm has converged.
23S1 Q3(b)用最短路算法找第二短路径5 分
Elaborate how to use the shortest path algorithm to find the second shortest path between a pair of source-destination nodes, while the second shortest path does not need to be link disjoint from the first shortest path. Note that the second shortest path may be of the same length as the first shortest path.
说明怎样用最短路径算法求一对源–目的节点之间的第二短路径。第二短路径不要求和最短路径链路不相交（可以共用链路）。注意：第二短路径的长度可能和最短路径一样。
参考答案
  1. 用 Dijkstra 求出源 s 到目的 t 的最短路 P，设它由链路 e1, e2, …, ek 组成。
  2. 第二短路径 P' ≠ P，所以 P' 至少不包含 P 的某一条链路 ei（否则 P' 就包含 P，作为无环路径只能等于 P）。
  3. 对 i = 1, 2, …, k：把 ei 从图中删掉（代价设为 ∞），在剩下的图上再跑一次 Dijkstra，得到 s 到 t 的最短路 Pi（可能不存在）。
  4. 在 P1, …, Pk 中取代价最小的一条，就是第二短路径。它的代价可能和 P 相同（原图有两条等长最短路时）。

这正是 P2A slide 64 "删掉一条链路后怎么重算"的思路，一共要跑 k + 1 次最短路算法。

English answer

  1. Run Dijkstra to find the shortest path P from source s to destination t; let P consist of links e1, e2, ..., ek.
  2. The second shortest path P' ≠ P, so P' must avoid at least one link ei of P (otherwise P' would contain P, and as a loop-free path it could only be equal to P).
  3. For i = 1, 2, ..., k: remove ei from the graph (set its cost to ∞) and run Dijkstra again on the remaining graph to obtain the shortest s-t path Pi (which may not exist).
  4. Among P1, ..., Pk, the one with the least cost is the second shortest path. Its cost may be the same as that of P (when the original graph has two equal-length shortest paths).

This is exactly the idea of P2A slide 64 (how to recompute after deleting a link); in total the shortest path algorithm is run k + 1 times.

PART II

传输层 · 往年 Q3/Q4（可靠传输部分）

不考 CA2 Quiz，期末考。下面是 5 份期末卷里属于 P2B slide 1–70（Week 10 讲的 UDP、rdt、停等/流水线、GBN）的小题，题干按原题参数用中文概述。最常见的两类：画 / 补 FSM，以及"一次发 3 个分组"的有效利用率（24S1、24S2、25S1 连续三年都有，15 分）。TCP 握手、关闭、Reno、流量控制、ECN 这几题等 TCP 讲完再补。

25S2 Q3(b)画停等协议的收发双方 FSM，序号要不要8 分 · FSM
停等协议；信道会有比特错误但不丢包；接收方只在正确收到分组时回 ACK，收到出错分组就保持沉默；发送方没按时收到正确 ACK 就超时重发，出错的 ACK 直接忽略（等超时）；两个方向的时延都很小，不会造成超时。分别画出发送方和接收方的 FSM。讨论这个方案是否需要序号并说明理由；如果需要，在 FSM 里明确画出来。
提示

想一个场景：分组正确到达、接收方已经交付并回了 ACK，但 ACK 出错了。发送方会怎么做？接收方收到的是什么？它分得清吗？

完整解答

序号：必须要（数据分组 1 位序号 0/1 就够）

ACK 出错时发送方忽略它、超时后重发同一个分组；而接收方已经交付过这个分组。没有序号，接收方没法区分"重传"和"新分组"，会把同一份数据交付两次，违反可靠传输。停等时同一时刻只有一个分组在途，序号 0、1 交替就足够。接收方收到重复分组时必须再回一次 ACK（否则发送方会一直超时重发）。ACK 本身可以不带序号：时延不会造成超时，不会出现"迟到的旧 ACK"；带上序号（ACK0/ACK1）也对，下面按带序号画。

发送方 FSM（4 个状态，和 rdt3.0 去掉丢包情形后一样）

状态事件动作下一状态
等上层调用 0（初始）rdt_send(data)sndpkt = make_pkt(0, data, checksum); udt_send(sndpkt); start_timer等 ACK0
等 ACK0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && corrupt(rcvpkt)Λ等 ACK0
等 ACK0timeoutudt_send(sndpkt); start_timer等 ACK0
等 ACK0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && isACK(rcvpkt, 0)stop_timer等上层调用 1
等上层调用 1、等 ACK1：同上，把 0 和 1 对调

接收方 FSM（2 个状态）

状态事件动作下一状态
等下层 0（初始）rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && has_seq0(rcvpkt)extract(rcvpkt, data); deliver_data(data); sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK0, chksum); udt_send(sndpkt)等下层 1
等下层 0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && corrupt(rcvpkt)Λ（保持沉默）等下层 0
等下层 0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && has_seq1(rcvpkt)sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK1, chksum); udt_send(sndpkt)（重复：不交付，再确认）等下层 0
等下层 1：同上，把 0 和 1 对调

考试时画成圆圈和箭头（事件写横线上、动作写横线下、虚线箭头标初始状态）。可以在学习手册的 RDT 演练场选 rdt3.0 对照形状。

English answer

Sequence numbers are required (a single 1-bit sequence number, 0/1, is enough for the data packets).

Consider this scenario: a packet is delivered correctly and the receiver sends back an ACK, but that ACK is corrupted. The sender ignores the corrupted ACK and, on timeout, retransmits the same packet — but the receiver has already delivered it. Without a sequence number the receiver cannot tell a retransmission apart from a new packet and would deliver the same data twice, which violates reliable data transfer. Because stop-and-wait only ever has one packet in flight, an alternating 0/1 sequence number is sufficient. When the receiver gets a duplicate packet it must still send back an ACK (otherwise the sender keeps timing out and retransmitting forever). The ACK itself does not strictly need a sequence number, since the delays are too small to ever cause a timeout, so there is no "stale/late ACK" problem; adding one (ACK0/ACK1) is equally acceptable and is what the table below shows.

Sender FSM (4 states, same shape as rdt3.0 with the "packet lost" transitions removed):

StateEventActionNext state
Wait for call 0 from above (initial)rdt_send(data)sndpkt = make_pkt(0, data, checksum); udt_send(sndpkt); start_timerWait for ACK0
Wait for ACK0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && corrupt(rcvpkt)Λ (do nothing)Wait for ACK0
Wait for ACK0timeoutudt_send(sndpkt); start_timerWait for ACK0
Wait for ACK0rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && isACK(rcvpkt, 0)stop_timerWait for call 1 from above
Wait for call 1 from above / Wait for ACK1: identical, with 0 and 1 swapped

Receiver FSM (2 states):

StateEventActionNext state
Wait for 0 from below (initial)rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && has_seq0(rcvpkt)extract(rcvpkt, data); deliver_data(data); sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK0, chksum); udt_send(sndpkt)Wait for 1 from below
Wait for 0 from belowrdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && corrupt(rcvpkt)Λ (stay silent)Wait for 0 from below
Wait for 0 from belowrdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && has_seq1(rcvpkt)sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK1, chksum); udt_send(sndpkt) (duplicate: do not deliver again, just re-ACK)Wait for 0 from below
Wait for 1 from below: identical, with 0 and 1 swapped

In the exam, draw this as circles and arrows (event above the line, action below the line, dashed arrow marking the initial state); you may compare shapes with rdt3.0 in the RDT simulator.

25S2 Q3(c)上题方案的期望有效利用率12 分 · 计算
沿用 3(b) 的方案。每个分组出错概率 10%，每个 ACK 出错概率 1%（重传也一样）；发一个分组用时 10 ms，发一个 ACK 用时 1 ms；传播、排队、处理时延忽略；超时 50 ms，从开始发送分组的时刻算起；发送方总有数据。有效利用率 = 平均每单位时间成功送达的数据（含首部）/ 信道容量，用百分数表示。求期望有效利用率。
提示

一次尝试成功 = 分组没错 且 ACK 没错。成功的尝试用时多少？失败的尝试（不管是分组错还是 ACK 错）用时多少？尝试次数服从几何分布。

完整解答
一次尝试成功的概率 p = 0.9 × 0.99 = 0.891 成功：发分组 10 ms + 回 ACK 1 ms = 11 ms 后就能发下一个 失败（分组错 → 接收方沉默；或 ACK 错 → 发送方忽略）：都要等到超时，从开始发送算起 50 ms 失败次数的期望 = (1 − p)/p = 0.109/0.891 = 0.1223 每成功送一个新分组的期望时间 = 0.1223 × 50 + 11 = 17.117 ms 其中有效的只有一次 10 ms 的发送 → U = 10 / 17.117 = 58.4%

易错：ACK 出错时数据其实已经送到了，但发送方不知道，仍然要超时重发；重发的那份是重复数据，不算有效数据。所以有效数据每个分组只算一次 10 ms。

English answer

One attempt succeeds only if the data packet is not corrupted and the ACK is not corrupted.

Probability a single attempt succeedsp = 0.9 × 0.99 = 0.891
Success casesend packet 10 ms + ACK back 1 ms = 11 ms, then the next packet can be sent
Failure case(packet corrupted → receiver stays silent; or ACK corrupted → sender ignores it): either way the sender must wait out the full timeout, measured from when it started sending → 50 ms
Expected number of failures before a success(1 − p)/p = 0.109/0.891 = 0.1223
Expected time to successfully deliver one new packet0.1223 × 50 + 11 = 17.117 ms
Expected utilisationonly one 10 ms transmission in that time is useful → U = 10 / 17.117 = 58.4%

Common mistake: when the ACK is corrupted the data has actually already arrived, but the sender does not know this and still retransmits after timeout. That retransmission delivers duplicate data and must not be counted again — each packet only contributes 10 ms of useful data, counted once.

25S1 Q3(b)一次 3 个分组 + 累积 ACK 的有效利用率15 分 · 计算
发送方连续发 3 个分组，然后停下来等 ACK 或超时。接收方收齐 3 个后只回一个累积 ACK："ack 1" 表示第 1 个对、第 2 个错，所以 2、3 都要重发；"ack 0" 表示 3 个全部重发。发送方收到 ACK 后马上从第一个需要重发的分组开始再发 3 个（例如收到 ack 1 就发 2、3、4）。数据分组 1250 字节，ACK 50 字节，两端速率 = 信道容量 = 1 Mbps，RTT = 10 ms。只有发送方向会出错和丢包：每个分组独立地有 10% 出错、1% 丢失（重传也一样）。3 个里只要丢一个，接收方就不回 ACK，发送方在发完 3 个后 100 ms 超时，重发这 3 个。处理时延忽略，发送方总有数据。求期望有效利用率。
提示

每一轮都是"发 3 个"，轮与轮之间相互独立，用"每轮期望有效分组数 ÷ 每轮期望时长"。没丢包时，这一轮真正前进几个分组 = 从头数连续正确的个数。

完整解答
发一个数据分组：1250 × 8 / 106 = 10 ms；ACK：50 × 8 / 106 = 0.4 ms 一轮先发 3 个：30 ms 3 个都没丢的概率 = 0.99³ = 0.970299：最后一位到达 30 + 5 = 35 ms，回 ACK 0.4 ms，再传 5 ms → 一轮 40.4 ms 至少丢一个（概率 0.029701）：没有 ACK，30 + 100 = 130 ms，前进 0 个 没丢时，这一轮前进的分组数 = 从头连续正确的个数，期望 = 0.9 + 0.9² + 0.9³ = 2.439 每轮期望前进 = 0.970299 × 2.439 = 2.3666 个分组 → 有效时间 23.666 ms 每轮期望时长 = 0.970299 × 40.4 + 0.029701 × 130 = 39.200 + 3.861 = 43.061 ms U = 23.666 / 43.061 = 55.0%

易错：① 前进的是"从头连续正确"的个数，不是正确的个数（例如 对-错-对 只前进 1 个，第 3 个也要重发）；② 丢包那一轮的时长是发完后再等 100 ms，不是 RTT；③ 出错和丢失按独立事件处理（没丢的分组再有 10% 出错）。

English answer

Data packet transmission time1250 × 8 / 106 = 10 ms
ACK transmission time50 × 8 / 106 = 0.4 ms
Time to send the 3 packets of a round30 ms
Probability none of the 3 is lost0.993 = 0.970299: last bit arrives at 30 + 5 = 35 ms, ACK takes 0.4 ms and propagates 5 ms → round length 40.4 ms
Probability at least one is lost0.029701: no ACK comes back, so 30 + 100 = 130 ms, and the round advances 0 packets
Packets advanced when nothing is lostequal to the number of consecutive correct packets counted from the first one; expectation = 0.9 + 0.92 + 0.93 = 2.439
Expected packets advanced per round0.970299 × 2.439 = 2.3666 packets → useful time 23.666 ms
Expected round length0.970299 × 40.4 + 0.029701 × 130 = 39.200 + 3.861 = 43.061 ms
UtilisationU = 23.666 / 43.061 = 55.0%

Common mistakes: (1) the window advances by the count of consecutive correct packets starting from the first, not simply the count of correct packets (e.g. correct–wrong–correct only advances by 1; the third packet must also be resent); (2) the length of a round where a packet is lost is "finish sending, then wait out the 100 ms timeout", not one RTT; (3) corruption and loss are independent events (a packet that was not lost can still independently suffer the 10% corruption chance).

24S2 Q3(b)一次 3 个分组 + 选择性重传的有效利用率15 分 · 计算
发送方连续发 3 个分组后停等。接收方收齐 3 个后回一个 ACK：要么肯定确认全部，要么指出哪几个出错（否定确认）；发送方只选择性重传出错的那几个，重传总是成功，接收方收到重传分组后立即肯定确认；这一组清空后才发下一组。数据分组 1500 字节，ACK/NAK 100 字节，1 Mbps，RTT = 5 ms。每个分组第一次发送时独立地有 10% 出错；不丢包；超时很长从不触发；处理时延忽略。求期望有效利用率。
提示

设第一轮有 k 个出错（二项分布）。k = 0 时这一组用多长时间？k ≥ 1 时要再加上什么？

完整解答
数据分组 1500 × 8 / 106 = 12 ms；ACK/NAK 100 × 8 / 106 = 0.8 ms；单程 2.5 ms 第一轮：36 ms 发完 + 0.8 ms 回 ACK/NAK + 5 ms 往返 = 41.8 ms 第一轮有 k 个出错：再发 k 个（12k ms），最后一个到达后回 ACK（0.8 ms），加往返 5 ms → 多 12k + 5.8 ms k ~ B(3, 0.1)：P(0) = 0.729，P(1) = 0.243，P(2) = 0.027，P(3) = 0.001 每组期望时长 = 41.8 + 12·E[k] + 5.8·P(k ≥ 1) = 41.8 + 12 × 0.3 + 5.8 × 0.271 = 46.972 ms 每组有效数据 = 3 个分组 = 36 ms → U = 36 / 46.972 = 76.6%

等价写法：0.729 × 41.8 + 0.243 × 59.6 + 0.027 × 71.6 + 0.001 × 83.6 = 46.972 ms。

English answer

Data packet / ACK-NAK transmission time1500 × 8 / 106 = 12 ms; 100 × 8 / 106 = 0.8 ms; one-way propagation 2.5 ms
First round36 ms to send the 3 packets + 0.8 ms for the ACK/NAK + 5 ms round-trip propagation = 41.8 ms
If k packets are in error in round 1resend those k packets (12k ms), then 0.8 ms for the ACK plus 5 ms round-trip once the last one arrives → an extra 12k + 5.8 ms
Distribution of kk ~ B(3, 0.1): P(0) = 0.729, P(1) = 0.243, P(2) = 0.027, P(3) = 0.001
Expected time per group of 341.8 + 12·E[k] + 5.8·P(k ≥ 1) = 41.8 + 12 × 0.3 + 5.8 × 0.271 = 46.972 ms
Useful data per group3 packets = 36 ms → U = 36 / 46.972 = 76.6%

Equivalent calculation: 0.729 × 41.8 + 0.243 × 59.6 + 0.027 × 71.6 + 0.001 × 83.6 = 46.972 ms.

24S1 Q3(b)一次 3 个分组，ACK 可能丢失15 分 · 计算
发送方连续发 3 个分组后停等 ACK；接收方 3 个全部收到才回一个 ACK。数据分组 1000 字节，ACK 50 字节，1 Mbps，RTT = 4 ms。发完 3 个后 40 ms 内没收到 ACK 就超时，重发这 3 个。处理时延忽略，信道没有其他流量，发送方总有数据。求两种情况下的有效利用率：(i) 数据和 ACK 都总是成功；(ii) 数据总是成功，但 ACK 有 10% 的概率丢失（重传后的 ACK 也一样），一直重传到收到 ACK 为止。
提示

(i) 画一轮的时序：发 3 个 → 最后一位到达 → 回 ACK → ACK 到达。(ii) 失败的一轮多长？期望失败几轮？

完整解答
3 个数据分组：3 × 1000 × 8 / 106 = 24 ms；ACK：50 × 8 / 106 = 0.4 ms；单程 2 ms (i) 一轮 = 24 + 2 + 0.4 + 2 = 28.4 ms → U = 24 / 28.4 = 84.5% (ii) ACK 丢了的一轮：24 ms 发完 + 40 ms 超时 = 64 ms，白费 期望失败轮数 = q/(1 − q) = 0.1/0.9 = 0.111 每成功一组的期望时间 = 0.111 × 64 + 28.4 = 35.511 ms U = 24 / 35.511 = 67.6%

易错：重传的 3 个分组虽然又成功送到，但接收方之前已经收到过，属于重复数据，有效数据每组只算一次 24 ms。

English answer

3 data packets3 × 1000 × 8 / 106 = 24 ms
ACK50 × 8 / 106 = 0.4 ms; one-way propagation 2 ms
(i) round length24 + 2 + 0.4 + 2 = 28.4 ms → U = 24 / 28.4 = 84.5%
(ii) a round where the ACK is lost24 ms to send + 40 ms timeout = 64 ms wasted
Expected number of failed roundsq/(1 − q) = 0.1/0.9 = 0.111
Expected time per successful group0.111 × 64 + 28.4 = 35.511 ms
(ii) utilisationU = 24 / 35.511 = 67.6%

Common mistake: the 3 packets that get retransmitted, although successfully delivered again, were already received by the receiver before — they are duplicate data, so the useful data for each group is still counted only once, as 24 ms.

24S1 Q3(c)画 GBN 接收方的 FSM5 分 · FSM
画出采用 Go-Back-N 的接收方的 FSM。只考虑最简单的 GBN：接收方总是对按序正确收到的最高序号回 ACK，不考虑扩展。
参考答案（P2B slide 63）

只有一个状态 Wait。虚线初始箭头上：事件 Λ；动作 expectedseqnum = 1，sndpkt = make_pkt(expectedseqnum, ACK, chksum)。

事件动作下一状态
rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && hasseqnum(rcvpkt, expectedseqnum)extract(rcvpkt, data)
deliver_data(data)
sndpkt = make_pkt(expectedseqnum, ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)
expectedseqnum++		Wait
defaultudt_send(sndpkt)　重发上一个 ACKWait

配上文字说明拿满分：只记 expectedseqnum；乱序分组直接丢弃、不缓存；总是对按序正确收到的最高序号回 ACK，所以会产生重复 ACK。

English answer

There is only a single state, "Wait". On the dashed initial arrow: event Λ; action expectedseqnum = 1, sndpkt = make_pkt(expectedseqnum, ACK, chksum).

EventActionNext state
rdt_rcv(rcvpkt) && notcorrupt(rcvpkt) && hasseqnum(rcvpkt, expectedseqnum)extract(rcvpkt, data)
deliver_data(data)
sndpkt = make_pkt(expectedseqnum, ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)
expectedseqnum++		Wait
defaultudt_send(sndpkt) (resend the last ACK)Wait

To get full marks, add the explanation: the receiver tracks only expectedseqnum; an out-of-order packet is discarded immediately and never buffered; it always ACKs the highest in-order sequence number correctly received so far, which is why duplicate ACKs occur.

23S1 Q3(c)补全停等 FSM 的 6 处动作6 分 · FSM
停等协议，传输会出错但不丢包（所以不需要超时）。图中两个状态"Wait for 0 from below"（初始）和"Wait for 1 from below"，事件都已给出，要求填出横线下的动作 (1)–(6)：(1) 0→1：notcorrupt 且 has_seq0；(2) 等 0 时自环：corrupt；(3) 等 1 时自环：corrupt；(4) 等 0 时自环：notcorrupt 且 has_seq1；(5) 等 1 时自环：notcorrupt 且 has_seq0；(6) 1→0：notcorrupt 且 has_seq1。
提示

题目文字写的是 sender，但"Wait for 0 from below"、has_seq0 都是接收方的东西：这就是 rdt2.1 接收方（slide 46）。

参考答案（P2B slide 46）
标号动作含义
(1)、(6)extract(rcvpkt, data)
deliver_data(data)
sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		收到期待的新分组：交付并确认，转到等另一个序号
(2)、(3)sndpkt = make_pkt(NAK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		出错：回 NAK
(4)、(5)sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		没出错但序号不对 = 重复分组：不交付，再回 ACK

如果按 rdt2.2（无 NAK）作答，(2)(4) 改成回 ACK1、(3)(5) 回 ACK0，(1) 回 ACK0、(6) 回 ACK1，也说得通；但图中把出错和重复分成两条自环，是 rdt2.1 的画法，回 NAK 最贴题。

English answer

Although the question text says "sender", the labels "Wait for 0 from below" and has_seq0 are in fact receiver-side elements: this is the rdt2.1 receiver (P2B slide 46).

LabelActionMeaning
(1), (6)extract(rcvpkt, data)
deliver_data(data)
sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		Expected new packet received: deliver it and ACK, then move to waiting for the other sequence number
(2), (3)sndpkt = make_pkt(NAK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		Corrupted: send a NAK
(4), (5)sndpkt = make_pkt(ACK, chksum)
udt_send(sndpkt)		Not corrupted but the wrong sequence number, i.e. a duplicate packet: do not deliver it again, just re-send the ACK

An answer using rdt2.2 (no NAK) also makes sense: (2)(4) become ACK1, (3)(5) become ACK0, (1) sends ACK0, (6) sends ACK1. But the diagram separates "corrupted" and "duplicate" into two distinct self-loops, which matches the rdt2.1 style, so answering with NAK fits the question best.

23S1 Q3(d)GBN 窗口 6：一次全成功 / 恰好重传一次的概率9 分 · 概率
Go-Back-N，N = 6。每个分组有 1% 在路上丢失；被接收方收到的分组又有 1% 被检出比特错误、必须重传；其余都成功，各分组相互独立。发送方当前有 6 个已发未确认分组。(i) 这 6 个全部无错无丢地被收到的概率？(ii) 恰好需要一次重传（重传第 3–6 个）之后 6 个才全部收到的概率？
提示

单个分组成功 = 没丢 且 没错。(ii) 要重传 3–6，说明第一轮里 1、2 成功、3 失败；4–6 第一轮的结果重要吗？

完整解答
单个分组成功：p = 0.99 × 0.99 = 0.9801 (i) 6 个全成功：p6 = 0.98016 = 0.8864 (ii) 第一轮 1、2 成功，3 失败：p² (1 − p)。GBN 接收方会丢掉 3 之后到达的所有分组，所以 4–6 第一轮成不成功都一样，都要重传 重传的 3–6 这 4 个全部成功：p4 P = p² × (1 − p) × p4 = p6 (1 − p) = 0.8864 × 0.0199 = 0.01764

易错：不要再乘 4–6 第一轮的成功概率——GBN 不缓存乱序分组，它们无论如何都被丢弃重发。这正是 GBN 和 SR 的区别。

English answer

A single packet succeeds only if it is neither lost nor corrupted.

Probability a single packet succeedsp = 0.99 × 0.99 = 0.9801
(i) all 6 succeedp6 = 0.98016 = 0.8864
(ii) packets 1, 2 succeed and packet 3 fails in round 1p2(1 − p). The GBN receiver discards every packet that arrives after the gap at packet 3, so it makes no difference whether packets 4–6 succeeded in round 1 or not — they must be retransmitted regardless
Retransmitted packets 3–6 all succeedp4
(ii) resultP = p2 × (1 − p) × p4 = p6(1 − p) = 0.8864 × 0.0199 = 0.01764

Common mistake: do not multiply in the round-1 success probability of packets 4–6 again — GBN never buffers out-of-order packets, so they are discarded and retransmitted no matter what happened to them in round 1. This is exactly the key difference between GBN and Selective Repeat.

25S1 Q4(a)网络射击游戏该用 TCP 还是 UDP5 分 · 论述
你在开发一款在线第一人称射击（FPS）游戏，要提供快节奏的射击体验、和队友的文字聊天、以及游戏内购买武器装备。应该用 TCP 还是 UDP？说明理由。
参考思路
  • 射击和移动（主体）→ UDP：位置、开火这些状态更新又小又频繁，时延最重要；丢掉一个更新没关系，下一个更新马上覆盖它。TCP 会为丢的那个分组重传，并且按序交付，后面的更新都被卡住等它（画面卡顿）；握手和拥塞控制还会增加时延、压低速率。
  • 文字聊天、内购 → 需要可靠：聊天不能丢字、不能乱序；购买涉及钱，必须准确、不能重复扣款。这部分适合 TCP（或在 UDP 上由应用层自己加确认、序号和重传，slide 22）。
  • 结论：游戏主体用 UDP，聊天和购买用 TCP（两个套接字并存）；如果只能选一个，选 UDP，并在应用层给聊天和购买消息加可靠机制。理由要落到"实时性 vs 可靠性"的取舍上。

English answer

  • Shooting and movement (the core gameplay) → UDP. Position and firing updates are small and frequent, and latency matters most; losing one update is not a problem because the next update overwrites it almost immediately. TCP would retransmit the lost segment and deliver data strictly in order, so every later update is stuck waiting behind it (visible stutter/freezing); the connection handshake and congestion control also add latency and reduce the achievable rate.
  • Text chat and in-game purchases → need reliability. Chat must not lose characters or deliver them out of order; purchases involve real money and must be accurate with no duplicate charges. This is a good fit for TCP (or the application itself could add acknowledgements, sequence numbers and retransmission on top of UDP, slide 22).
  • Conclusion: use UDP for the game's core traffic and TCP for chat and purchases (two sockets running side by side). If only one protocol can be chosen, pick UDP and add application-layer reliability mechanisms for the chat and purchase messages. The justification should rest on the trade-off between real-time responsiveness and reliability.
章节
TOPIC 1

导论：分组交换与时延

T1 · slide 27–2835 人和 100 人共享 1 Mbps统计复用
1 Mbps 链路，每个用户活跃时 100 kbps，10% 时间活跃。(a) 电路交换能支持几人？(b) 分组交换 35 人时，同时活跃超过 10 人的概率？(c) 100 人时呢？
完整解答
(a) 1 Mbps / 100 kbps = 10(b) 1 − Σn=010 C(35,n)·0.1n·0.935−n = 0.000424（讲义写作 < 0.0004 / ≈ 0.0004）(c) 1 − Σn=010 C(100,n)·0.1n·0.9100−n = 0.417

100 人时平均就有 10 人活跃，正好等于容量，所以有四成时间拥塞："贪心"多放人会让分组交换的优势消失。

English answer

(a)1 Mbps / 100 kbps = 10
(b)1 − Σn=010 C(35,n)·0.1n·0.935−n = 0.000424 (the notes write this as < 0.0004 / ≈ 0.0004)
(c)1 − Σn=010 C(100,n)·0.1n·0.9100−n = 0.417

With 100 users, on average exactly 10 are active at once — exactly equal to the link's capacity — so the link is congested almost 40% of the time: being "greedy" and admitting too many users this way wipes out packet switching's statistical-multiplexing advantage.

T1 · slide 31传输时延 vs 传播时延时延
分组 1 KB（按 8000 bit 计）。情形 1：R = 1 Gbps，100 km 光纤。情形 2：R = 100 Mbps，100 m 铜缆。传播速度 2×108 m/s。分别求 P/R 和 T（传播时延），单位 µs。
完整解答
情形 1：8000/109 = 8 µs；105/(2×108) = 500 µs → T ≫ P/R情形 2：8000/108 = 80 µs；100/(2×108) = 0.5 µs → T ≪ P/R

English answer

P/R (transmission delay)T (propagation delay)Comparison
Case 1: R = 1 Gbps, 100 km fibre8000/109 = 8 µs105/(2×108) = 500 µsT ≫ P/R
Case 2: R = 100 Mbps, 100 m copper8000/108 = 80 µs100/(2×108) = 0.5 µsT ≪ P/R
T1 · slide 368000 字节文件过 3 条 1 Mbps 链路存储转发
忽略首部、处理、传播、排队。(a) 整个文件作为一个分组；(b) 切成 1000 字节的分组。到达 B 各要多少 ms？
完整解答（Session 2 补充 slide 3）
(a) Tm = L·M/R = 3 × 64 000 / 106 = 192 ms(b) T = 8000/106 = 8 ms，N = 8：D = L·T + (N − 1)·T = 24 + 56 = 80 ms

English answer

Header, processing, propagation and queueing delay are all ignored.

(a) whole file as one packetTm = L·M/R = 3 × 64 000 / 106 = 192 ms
(b) split into 1000-byte packetsT = 8000/106 = 8 ms, N = 8: D = L·T + (N − 1)·T = 24 + 56 = 80 ms
T1 · Session 2 扩展加上首部、处理和传播时延存储转发
同上，但首部 80 字节，每台中间节点处理一个分组 1 ms，每条链路传播 10 ms。(a) 一个大分组；(b) 1000 字节分组。
完整解答（Session 2 补充 slide 5–6）
(a) Tm = 3 × (64 000 + 640)/106 = 193.92；加 2 × 1 + 3 × 10 → 225.92 ms(b) Tpacket = 8640/106 = 8.64 ms；第一个到达 3 × 8.64 + 30 + 2 = 57.92；再加 7 × 8.64 = 60.48 → 118.4 ms

English answer

Same setup, but now with an 80-byte header, 1 ms of processing delay at each intermediate node, and 10 ms of propagation delay on each link.

(a) one big packetTm = 3 × (64 000 + 640)/106 = 193.92 ms; adding 2 × 1 ms processing + 3 × 10 ms propagation → 225.92 ms
(b) 1000-byte packetsTpacket = 8640/106 = 8.64 ms; the first packet arrives at 3 × 8.64 + 30 + 2 = 57.92 ms; adding the remaining 7 × 8.64 = 60.48 ms → 118.4 ms

T2.1 · Example 1停等达到 50% 效率的帧长停等

完整解答

0.5 = tf/(tf + 40 ms) → tf = 40 ms
L = 4000 × 0.04 = 160 bit = 20 bytes
T2.1 · Example 21 Gbps、15 ms 的停等效率停等
信道 1 Gbps，传播 15 ms，帧 1000 字节。停等效率和吞吐量？

T2.1 · Example 3卫星信道不同窗口的吞吐量滑动窗口
无差错 2 Mbps 卫星信道，16 KB 数据帧，ACK 很短，往返传播时延 540 ms。窗口 1、7、15、128 时的最大吞吐量（本课 k = 10³，16 KB = 16 000 字节）。
完整解答
tf = 128 000 / (2×106) = 64 ms；540 ms 已是往返 2tprop；2a = 8.44；2a + 1 = 9.44W = 1：1/9.44 × 2 Mbps = 212 kbpsW = 7：7/9.44 × 2 Mbps = 1.483 MbpsW = 15、128 ≥ 9.44 → 满载 2 Mbps

老师课上的算法。若按 16 384 字节：216 kbps、1.515 Mbps。

English answer

tf = 128 000 / (2×106) = 64 ms; the given 540 ms is already the round-trip 2tprop; 2a = 8.44; 2a + 1 = 9.44
W = 1: 1/9.44 × 2 Mbps = 212 kbps
W = 7: 7/9.44 × 2 Mbps = 1.483 Mbps
W = 15, 128 ≥ 9.44 → fully loaded, 2 Mbps

This follows the lecturer's in-class method. If 16 384 bytes is used instead of 16 000: 216 kbps and 1.515 Mbps.

T2.1 · slide 53单奇偶校验的漏检概率奇偶校验
n = 32 位码字，比特错误率 p = 10−3，独立出错。单奇偶校验检测不到错误的概率约是多少？
讲义近似 4.96，精确 4.81，都算对。
完整解答
漏检 = 偶数个错误：C(32,2)p²(1−p)30 + C(32,4)p⁴(1−p)28 + …讲义近似（忽略 (1−p) 因子）：496×10−6 + 35 960×10−12 ≈ 4.96×10−4，约每 2000 个错误漏 1 个精确值 4.81×10−4

English answer

An undetected error requires an even number of bit errors:

C(32,2)p2(1−p)30 + C(32,4)p4(1−p)28 + …

The notes' approximation (dropping the (1−p) factors): 496×10−6 + 35 960×10−12 ≈ 4.96×10−4 — roughly 1 in every 2000 errors goes undetected.

The exact value is 4.81×10−4.

T2.1 · slide 59 + Session 3三个 Checksum 例子Checksum
(a) 16 位字 1000000110111100 和 0100100001011010；(b) 4 位字 1000、1010、0000、1001；(c) 8 位字 10001010、00001001。求各自的 checksum。
完整解答
(a) 81BCH + 485AH = 33212 + 18522 = 51734；65535 − 51734 = 13801 = 35E9H = 0011 0101 1110 1001(b) 8 + 10 + 0 + 9 = 27；27 mod 15 = 12；15 − 12 = 3 = 0011(c) 138 + 9 = 147；255 − 147 = 108 = 01101100

English answer

(a)81BCH + 485AH = 33212 + 18522 = 51734; 65535 − 51734 = 13801 = 35E9H = 0011 0101 1110 1001
(b)8 + 10 + 0 + 9 = 27; 27 mod 15 = 12; 15 − 12 = 3 = 0011
(c)138 + 9 = 147; 255 − 147 = 108 = 01101100
T2.1 · slide 69–71CRC 发送与接收CRC
(a) 信息 1100，C(x) = x³ + x + 1，求码字。(b) 信息 110111，C(x) = 101，求码字。(c) 收到 11001101（C = 101），余数是多少？
完整解答
(a) 1100000 ÷ 1011：商 1110，余数 010 → 1100 010，即 x⁶ + x⁵ + x(b) 11011100 ÷ 101 → 余数 01 → 110111 01(c) 11001101 ÷ 101 → 余数 1 ≠ 0 → 有错

English answer

(a)1100000 ÷ 1011: quotient 1110, remainder 010 → codeword 1100 010, i.e. x6 + x5 + x
(b)11011100 ÷ 101 → remainder 01 → codeword 110111 01
(c)11001101 ÷ 101 → remainder 1 ≠ 0 → error detected
T2.1 · Session 4证明 E(x) = x⁸ + x² 检测不到（g = x⁴ + x² + 1）CRC 性质
生成多项式 g(x) = x⁴ + x² + 1。用"x 的幂次除以 g(x) 的余数"表证明错误 E(x) = x⁸ + x² 检测不到。x⁶ mod g(x) 是什么？
完整解答
x⁰..x³ 余数是自己；x⁴ ≡ x² + 1；x⁵ ≡ x³ + x；x⁶ ≡ x⁴ + x² ≡ (x² + 1) + x² = 1所以余数每 6 个一循环：x⁸ ≡ x²rem(x⁸) + rem(x²) = x² + x² = 0 → g(x) 整除 E(x) → 检测不到

这说明 g(x) 整除 x⁶ + 1，码字长度超过 6 时，间隔为 6 的双比特错误都检测不到（性质 P3 不满足）。

English answer

x0..x3 have themselves as the remainder; x4 ≡ x2 + 1; x5 ≡ x3 + x; x6 ≡ x4 + x2 ≡ (x2 + 1) + x2 = 1.

So the remainders repeat with period 6, which means x8 ≡ x2.

rem(x8) + rem(x2) = x2 + x2 = 0 → g(x) divides E(x) → the error is undetected.

This shows g(x) divides x6 + 1; for codewords longer than 6 bits, any double-bit error whose two error positions are 6 apart will go undetected (property P3 is not satisfied).

T2.2 · slide 18 / 21ALOHA 与时隙 ALOHA 的终端数ALOHA

Slotted ALOHA2400 × 0.368 = 883.2 bps → 530 terminals

T2.2 · slide 53–61五个网桥的生成树STP
LAN1 上有 B1(1)、B2(1)、B3(1)；LAN2 上有 B1(2)、B2(2)、B4(1)；LAN3 上有 B3(2)、B4(2)、B5(1)；LAN4 上有 B3(3)、B5(2)。经过一个 LAN 代价 1。哪些端口被阻塞？
完整解答
  1. B1 ID 最小 → 根。
  2. 根端口：B2、B3 都是端口 1（经 LAN1 代价 1）；B4 端口 1（经 LAN2 代价 1）；B5 端口 1（经 LAN3 代价 2）。
  3. 指定网桥：LAN1、LAN2 → B1（代价 0）；LAN3 → B3 和 B4 代价都是 1，选 ID 小的 B3；LAN4 → B3（代价 1）比 B5（代价 2）小。
  4. 转发：B1(1)(2)、B2(1)、B3(1)(2)(3)、B4(1)、B5(1)。阻塞：B2(2)、B4(2)、B5(2)。

English answer

  1. The bridge with the smallest ID is B1 → it becomes the root.
  2. Root ports: B2 and B3 both use port 1 (cost 1, via LAN1); B4 uses port 1 (cost 1, via LAN2); B5 uses port 1 (cost 2, via LAN3).
  3. Designated bridges: LAN1 and LAN2 → B1 (cost 0); on LAN3, B3 and B4 are tied at cost 1, so the one with the smaller ID, B3, is chosen; on LAN4, B3 (cost 1) beats B5 (cost 2).
  4. Forwarding ports: B1(1)(2), B2(1), B3(1)(2)(3), B4(1), B5(1). Blocked ports: B2(2), B4(2), B5(2).
TOPIC 3

网络层与 IP

T3 · slide 13最长前缀匹配转发
转发表：11001000 00010111 00010*** → 0；11001000 00010111 00011000 → 1；11001000 00010111 00011*** → 2；otherwise → 3。DA1 = 11001000 00010111 00010110 10100001，DA2 = 11001000 00010111 00011000 10101010，各从哪个接口转发？
完整解答

DA1 第三字节 00010110 只匹配第一行（前 5 位 00010）→ 0。DA2 第三字节 00011000 同时匹配第二行（24 位）和第三行（21 位），取最长 → 1。

English answer

DA1's third byte, 00010110, matches only the first row (first 5 bits 00010 match) → interface 0.

DA2's third byte, 00011000, matches both the second row (24-bit match) and the third row (21-bit match); the longest match wins → interface 1.

T3 · Example 4–5由 offset 求字节编号分片
一个分片 offset = 100，HLEN = 5，Total length = 100。第一个和最后一个数据字节的编号？
完整解答（Session 6 补充 slide 7–8）

第一个字节 = 100 × 8 = 800。首部 5 × 4 = 20 字节，数据 100 − 20 = 80 字节，最后一个字节 = 800 + 80 − 1 = 879。只知道 offset 时无法知道最后一个字节。

English answer

First byte number = 100 × 8 = 800.

Header length = 5 × 4 = 20 bytes, so data carried = 100 − 20 = 80 bytes, and the last byte number = 800 + 80 − 1 = 879.

Note: the offset field alone cannot tell you the last byte number — the total length is also needed, which is why it is given here.

T3 · Example 61484 字节数据进 MTU 576 的网络分片
ID = 63，首部 20 字节，数据 1484 字节，MTU 576。写出各片 Total length、MF、Offset。
完整解答

每片数据最多 556 → 552。1484 = 552 + 552 + 380。三片：572/MF 1/offset 0；572/1/69；400/0/138。

English answer

Each fragment can carry at most 556 bytes of data, rounded down to a multiple of 8 → 552 bytes. 1484 = 552 + 552 + 380, so there are three fragments:

FragmentTotal lengthMFOffset
157210
2572169
34000138
T3 · Example 9B 类网络要 50 个子网子网
B 类 176.56.0.0 要 50 个子网（不用全 0/全 1 子网）。子网掩码和每个子网的主机数？
完整解答（Session 6 补充 slide 9）

2⁶ − 2 = 62 ≥ 50 → 借 6 位，掩码 11111111.11111111.11111100.00000000 = 255.255.252.0（/22）；主机 10 位 → 2¹⁰ − 2 = 1022。

English answer

26 − 2 = 62 ≥ 50 → borrow 6 bits for the subnet field.

Subnet mask: 11111111.11111111.11111100.00000000 = 255.255.252.0 (a /22).

That leaves 10 host bits per subnet → 210 − 2 = 1022 usable hosts per subnet.
