PART II

网络层 II（P2A slides）

P2A · slide 22两端口和三端口的 HOL 吞吐量路由器
输入排队的交换机，队首分组的目的均匀随机。(a) 2 个输入/输出端口时，两个队首目的相同的概率？平均吞吐量？(b) 3 个端口时的平均吞吐量？
完整解答
(a) 第二个队首和第一个同目的的概率 1/2；吞吐量 = (1/2 × 1 + 1/2 × 2)/2 = 0.75(b) 3³ = 27 种组合：3 种全同（送 1），6 种全不同（送 3），18 种两个不同目的（送 2）吞吐量 = (3 + 18 + 36)/27/3 = 57/81 = 0.704

English answer

(a) The probability that the second HOL packet has the same destination as the first is 1/2.

Throughput = (1/2 × 1 + 1/2 × 2) / 2 = 0.75

(b) There are 33 = 27 combinations of HOL destinations:

  • 3 in which all three are the same (1 packet sent);
  • 6 in which all three are different (3 packets sent);
  • 18 with two different destinations (2 packets sent).

Throughput = (3 + 18 + 36) / 27 / 3 = 57/81 = 0.704

P2A · slide 26前瞻调度路由器
4×4 交换。输入 1 的队列：队首去输出 4，第二个去输出 2；输入 2 的队列：队首去输出 4。用"看前 2 个分组"的前瞻调度，这一时隙送出哪些（输入-输出）对？只用 FIFO 呢，送出几个？
完整解答

两个队首都要去输出 4，只能送一个，第一轮调度 2-4。输入 1 没送出但还有分组，看它的第二个分组（去输出 2），输出 2 空闲，调度 1-2。共送出 2 个。纯 FIFO 只能送 1 个，输入 1 的第二个分组被队首挡住（HOL 阻塞）。

English answer

  • Both HOL packets are destined to output 4, so only one can be sent; the first round schedules 2-4.
  • Input 1 has not sent a packet but still has packets queued, so it looks at its second packet (to output 2); output 2 is idle, so 1-2 is scheduled.
  • In total 2 packets are sent.
  • With pure FIFO only 1 packet can be sent: the second packet at input 1 is blocked by the packet at the head of its queue (head-of-the-line (HOL) blocking).
P2A · slide 40Dijkstra：节点 1 到其他节点Dijkstra
链路：1-2 (1)、1-4 (4)、2-3 (3)、2-5 (1)、3-5 (1)、3-6 (2)、4-5 (1)、5-6 (4)。从节点 1 出发求 D3、D4、D6，以及第二、第三个被着色的节点。
完整解答
初始 P = {1}：D2 = 1，D4 = 4，其余 ∞着色 2（D = 1）：D3 = 1 + 3 = 4，D5 = 1 + 1 = 2着色 5（D = 2）：D3 = min(4, 2 + 1) = 3，D4 = min(4, 2 + 1) = 3，D6 = 2 + 4 = 6着色 3 和 4（D = 3）：D6 = min(6, 3 + 2) = 5着色 6，结束。最短路树：1-2-5-3-6，1-2-5-4

English answer

  • Initially P = {1}: D2 = 1, D4 = 4, all others ∞.
  • Colour node 2 (D = 1): D3 = 1 + 3 = 4, D5 = 1 + 1 = 2.
  • Colour node 5 (D = 2): D3 = min(4, 2 + 1) = 3, D4 = min(4, 2 + 1) = 3, D6 = 2 + 4 = 6.
  • Colour nodes 3 and 4 (D = 3): D6 = min(6, 3 + 2) = 5.
  • Colour node 6; the algorithm terminates. Shortest-path tree: 1-2-5-3-6 and 1-2-5-4.
P2A · slide 44–45Dijkstra 表格与转发表Dijkstra
u-v 2、u-x 1、u-w 5、v-x 2、v-w 3、x-w 3、x-y 1、w-y 1、w-z 5、y-z 2。从 u 出发：D(w)、D(z) 最终是多少？w 的前驱？u 去 z 走哪条出链路？
完整解答

按 slide 44 的表：N' 依次为 u → ux → uxy → uxyv → uxyvw → uxyvwz。D(w) 从 5,u 变成 4,x 再变成 3,y；D(z) 在 y 着色时变成 4,y。最短路树中 v 直连 u，其他节点都经 x。转发表：v → (u,v)，x、y、w、z → (u,x)。

English answer

  • Following the table on slide 44, N' grows as u → ux → uxy → uxyv → uxyvw → uxyvwz.
  • D(w),p(w) changes from 5,u to 4,x and then to 3,y; D(z),p(z) becomes 4,y when y is added to N'.
  • In the least-cost path tree, v is connected directly to u, and all other nodes are reached via x.
  • Forwarding table at u: v → (u,v); x, y, w, z → (u,x).
P2A · slide 49, 53–56Bellman-Ford：到节点 6DV
同一张图（1-2 3、1-3 2、1-4 5、2-4 1、2-5 4、3-4 2、3-6 1、4-5 3、5-6 2），目的节点 6。第 2 轮节点 2 的表项是什么？最终节点 2 的下一跳和距离？
完整解答

第 1 轮只有节点 3 (6,1) 和节点 5 (6,2)。第 2 轮节点 2 的邻居里只有 5 有值：4 + 2 = 6 → (5, 6)；同时节点 1、4 得到 (3,3)。第 3 轮节点 2 经 4：1 + 3 = 4，经 1：3 + 3 = 6，经 5：4 + 2 = 6 → (4, 4)（slide 49 的计算）。

English answer

  • Iteration 1: only node 3 (6, 1) and node 5 (6, 2) have finite entries.
  • Iteration 2: among node 2's neighbours, only node 5 has a value: 4 + 2 = 6 → (5, 6); at the same time nodes 1 and 4 obtain (3, 3).
  • Iteration 3: node 2 via node 4: 1 + 3 = 4; via node 1: 3 + 3 = 6; via node 5: 4 + 2 = 6 → (4, 4) (the calculation on slide 49).
P2A · slide 57–61距离向量、好消息与坏消息DV
(a) x-y 2、y-z 1、x-z 7，x 收到 y、z 的距离向量后 Dx(z) = ？(b) x-y 4、y-z 1、x-z 50，x-y 突然变成 60（无毒性逆转）。y 第一次重算时认为自己到 x 的距离是多少？(c) 加上毒性逆转后呢？
完整解答
(a) Dx(z) = min{c(x,y) + Dy(z), c(x,z) + Dz(z)} = min{2 + 1, 7 + 0} = 3(b) z 上次说自己到 x 是 5 → y：min{60, 1 + 5} = 6（经 z），其实 z 的 5 本身就是经过 y 的，于是开始无穷计数(c) z 经 y 到 x，所以 z 告诉 y 自己到 x 是 ∞ → y：min{60, 1 + ∞} = 60（直连）；之后 z 改为直连 50，y 再改为经 z 的 51

English answer

(a) Dx(z) = min{c(x,y) + Dy(z), c(x,z) + Dz(z)} = min{2 + 1, 7 + 0} = 3

(b) z last advertised that its distance to x is 5, so y computes min{60, 1 + 5} = 6 (via z). But z's cost of 5 is itself a path through y, so the count-to-infinity problem begins.

(c) With poisoned reverse: z routes to x via y, so z tells y that its distance to x is ∞, and y computes min{60, 1 + ∞} = 60 (direct link). Afterwards z switches to its direct link (cost 50), and y then changes to 51 via z.

P2A · slide 77–79RIP 的几个数协议
RIP：最大跳数？表示无穷的值？更新周期（秒）？多少秒收不到邻居更新就认为链路断了？用 UDP 哪个端口？
完整解答

最大 15 跳，16 = 无穷；每 30 s 发更新；180 s 内没收到邻居 X 的更新就认为到 X 的链路失效，代价设为 16；由应用层进程 routed 管理，跑在 UDP 520 端口上。检测到变化会立即通知邻居。

English answer

  • Maximum path length is 15 hops; 16 = infinity.
  • Advertisements (updates) are sent every 30 s.
  • If no update is heard from neighbour X within 180 s, the link to X is declared failed and its cost is set to 16.
  • RIP is managed by an application-level process called routed, running over UDP port 520.
  • When a change is detected, the neighbours are notified immediately.
P2A · slide 88–96BGP 路由选择与策略协议
(a) BGP 有多条路由时依次按哪些准则选？(b) B 从 A 收到路径 AW，B 应不应该把 BAW 通告给提供商 C？为什么？(c) 双归属的末梢网络 X 为什么不向 B 通告到 C 的路由？
参考答案
  1. (a) ① 本地偏好值（策略）；② 最短 AS-PATH；③ 最近的 NEXT-HOP 路由器（热土豆）；④ 其他准则。
  2. (b) 不应该。W 和 C 都不是 B 的客户，B 替 C 转发去 W 的流量（路径 CBAW）没有收入；B 只想为自己的客户转发，所以不通告，逼 C 经 A 去 W。
  3. (c) X 不想当中转网络：如果 X 向 B 通告到 C 的路由，B 去 C 的流量可能经过 X，消耗 X 的资源。BGP 路径选择由策略决定，而不是路径最优。

English answer

  1. (a) In order: (1) local preference value (policy decision); (2) shortest AS-PATH; (3) closest NEXT-HOP router (hot potato routing); (4) additional criteria.
  2. (b) No. Neither W nor C is B's customer, so B earns no revenue for forwarding C's traffic to W (path CBAW). B only wants to carry traffic to/from its own customers, so it does not advertise BAW to C, forcing C to reach W via A.
  3. (c) X does not want to act as a transit network: if X advertised to B a route to C, B's traffic to C might pass through X and consume X's resources. BGP route selection is determined by policy, not by path optimality.
P2A · slide 117OpenFlow 例子：h5、h6 经 s1、s2 到 h3SDN
h5、h6（10.3.0.5、10.3.0.6）接在 s3 上；要求它们发往 10.2.*.* 的数据报经 s1 再到 s2，送到 h3（10.2.0.3，s2 端口 3）。s3 的 3 号端口接 s1，s1 的 4 号端口接 s2（s2 的 2 号端口）。各交换机的动作是 forward 到几号端口？
完整解答

s3：match IP Src = 10.3.*.*、IP Dst = 10.2.*.* → forward(3)。s1：match ingress port = 1、IP Src = 10.3.*.*、IP Dst = 10.2.*.* → forward(4)。s2：match ingress port = 2、IP Dst = 10.2.0.3 → forward(3)；IP Dst = 10.2.0.4 → forward(4)。按源地址区分路径，正是传统基于目的地址的转发做不到的。

English answer

  • s3: match IP Src = 10.3.*.*, IP Dst = 10.2.*.* → action forward(3).
  • s1: match ingress port = 1, IP Src = 10.3.*.*, IP Dst = 10.2.*.* → action forward(4).
  • s2: match ingress port = 2, IP Dst = 10.2.0.3 → forward(3); IP Dst = 10.2.0.4 → forward(4).
  • The path is selected according to the source address, which traditional destination-based forwarding cannot do.
P2A · slide 64四个思考题算法应用
(1) 删掉一条链路要重算所有最短路吗？(2) 加一条链路呢？(3) 要求路径上每条链路容量不低于某个下限怎么办？(4) 路径不能经过某个节点怎么办？
参考答案
  1. 不用。只有最短路树用到该链路的那部分目的节点受影响；链路不在树上，源的最短路全都不变。
  2. 新链路 (i,j) 代价 c：若 D(i) + c ≥ D(j) 且 D(j) + c ≥ D(i)，什么都不变；否则只从变短的那一端开始更新受影响的节点。
  3. 先删掉所有容量低于下限的链路，再在剩下的图上跑最短路。
  4. 删掉该节点和它的所有链路（代价设为 ∞），再跑最短路。

English answer

  1. No. Only the destination nodes whose paths in the shortest-path tree use that link are affected; if the link is not on the tree, all of the source's shortest paths remain unchanged.
  2. For a new link (i,j) with cost c: if D(i) + c ≥ D(j) and D(j) + c ≥ D(i), nothing changes; otherwise, update the affected nodes starting only from the end whose distance becomes shorter.
  3. First delete all links whose capacity is below the lower bound, then run the shortest path algorithm on the remaining graph.
  4. Delete that node and all of its links (set their costs to ∞), then run the shortest path algorithm.
